16:I[44692,["/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/08cykig6vtx_p.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/07qi2okp-.yp9.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0frujd.eyhz4o.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0n4z.7yu76je-.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/09-o.77-p0p36.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/1304s0wcp2kce.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0j1uy9t~zxcf0.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/14_5o-nbg8k_r.js"],"DailyView"]
17:T471,ClosureBench is built around “logic primitives + executable semantics + controlled instance generation.”
- Task space: 26 categories derived from explicit graph-relational primitives such as reachability/transitive closure, degree, set operations, connectivity (e.g., SCC-related queries), and aggregation over reachable sets.
- Exact labels: each query is implemented as a short program whose execution yields the reference answer. The paper mentions implementations in Ein (tensor-contraction style graph ops) and an equivalent Python/NetworkX version.
- Constructive generator: instances are produced on demand with separate structural and surface seeds, allowing independent control of graph structure and linguistic realization.
- Difficulty control: three orthogonal axes—number of nodes, edge density, and query depth/compositional level—so evaluators can hold two fixed and sweep the third to localize failure modes.
- Interface: models only see natural-language questions and must output JSON; the underlying program is hidden, keeping the benchmark focused on reasoning rather than program induction from code exposure.18:T4ab,The experiments use code-generation tasks because hidden unit tests provide an exact oracle while remaining unavailable to the operators. The data comes from MBPP+ and HumanEval+. The loop runs on 75 MBPP+ training tasks. Evaluation uses 428 unseen tasks: 75 held-out MBPP+ tasks, 228 additional MBPP+ tasks, and 125 HumanEval+ tasks. A naive operator-generation loop is highly redundant: five runs produce 183 flagging operators but only 96 distinct flag sets. Scoring all 295 authored operators at the narrow interface yields no discovery. EvalCEGAR admits an operator in six of eight runs, and all six admitted operators help on the 428 unseen tasks. The best generated operator is 55 lines of Python and matches the best hand-written operator on a second benchmark while using about one third as many flags. Ablations show that changing the admission question alone moves admissions from zero to one, while the narrow interface admits nothing in 336 attempts. For composition, the authors enumerate about 2.3M subsets and show that the loop’s original composition objective can rank near the 5th percentile, whereas an untuned class-weighted objective reaches the 91st percentile or above.19:T4e6,SESSE is a five-stage pipeline. Stage 0 runs the judge holistically on a development set and collects examples where the judge disagrees with the reference label. Stage 1 prompts the same judge to derive conditional evaluation sub-questions from those errors, using a format such as: if a condition applies, which response better satisfies a given property, A, B, or NA. These criteria require neither human-written rubrics nor oracle responses. Stage 2 embeds the candidate questions and clusters them into k thematic groups. Stage 3 ranks questions within each cluster using greedy farthest-first traversal, producing a diversity-ordered bank that can be truncated at different depths. Stage 4 asks an LLM to summarize each cluster into an abstract protasis and merges near-duplicate clusters through binary equivalence checks. Stage 5 first selects relevant clusters for a new example, then answers selected concrete sub-questions with A/B/NA votes. NA votes are ignored; A/B majority gives the final preference; ties are abstentions and are excluded from the accuracy denominator. The key distinction from prior rubric or specialist-judge methods is that the criteria are induced from the judge’s own failure distribution without parameter updates.1a:T590,The method has three main pieces. First, a library of 14 semantics-preserving transformations, including control-flow rewrites, dead-code injection, and identifier renaming. These are meant to cover both realistic refactorings and stress-test perturbations. Semantic preservation is operationalized through per-test equivalence on the project test suite rather than formal program equivalence, which is pragmatic and aligned with SWE-bench acceptance. Second, a randomized variant sampler applies selected transformations at sampled locations to create independent perturbed repositories. This is intentionally not an adversarial search procedure. The point is to show that even simple, unguided local rewrites already hurt agents, so the measured effect is a lower bound. Third, the evaluation design explicitly handles agent stochasticity by repeating runs on both the original seed repo and its perturbed variants and comparing them in a paired manner. The study also tracks not only issue resolution rate but step count and token cost. Experiments cover two scaffolds, mini-SWE agent and OpenCode, with four frontier models: Claude Opus 4.5, Kimi K2.5, MiniMax M2.5, and Qwen 3.6-27B, on 54 instances from SWE-bench Verified and SWE-bench Pro. The strongest contribution is the evaluation methodology: robustness is measured over a semantic-equivalence neighborhood, not as a single score on a fixed repository snapshot.1b:T543,The main findings are clear. First, simple local non-adversarial perturbations cause measurable degradation in most settings, with the worst configuration dropping by up to 6.7 percentage points in resolve rate; 6 out of 16 model-scaffold-benchmark configurations show statistically significant degradation. Second, even when solve rate is mostly unchanged, agents often work harder: step count increases by up to 9.9% and token cost by up to 22.9%. This means pass/fail alone misses part of the robustness problem. Third, higher baseline capability does not imply higher robustness. Within a scaffold, stronger models on the unperturbed benchmark can be more brittle than weaker ones. Across scaffolds, the simpler scaffold is often more robust. Fourth, robustness rankings are unstable across scaffolds and benchmarks. The paper highlights Qwen as relatively robust under mini-SWE agent on SWE-bench Verified but the most brittle under OpenCode. Fifth, the effect is highly concentrated in a small subset of repositories or instances rather than spread uniformly. That suggests future work should look at repository structure, test coverage, naming style, and retrieval/edit trajectories as moderators. The sample size is still modest, but the repeated paired-run design makes the conclusions more credible than one-shot leaderboard comparisons.1c:T472,Start with the introduction to frame the problem correctly: this is about agent robustness under semantics-preserving repository rewrites, not generic code-model robustness. Then read the SPT section and the table listing the 14 transformations to understand what kinds of surface variation are being tested. The most important part is the experimental design: how the paper separates perturbation effects from intrinsic run-to-run variance, and why it chooses randomized non-adversarial sampling instead of attack-based search. In the results, focus on three groups of figures or tables: resolve-rate degradation, step/token overhead, and the breakdown by scaffold, benchmark, and repository. Those are where the “jagged frontier” claim becomes concrete. A careful skim should take about 25 to 35 minutes. If reading more deeply, two follow-up questions are worth keeping in mind: how much brittleness comes from the pretrained code representation versus the agent scaffold, and whether robustness-oriented augmentation during code pretraining or agent training would improve both reliability and efficiency or introduce a trade-off.1d:T587,The contribution is the benchmark design. FM-Bench is a deterministic football-management simulator where an LLM agent operates through 26 schema-generated tools. Across roughly 340 to 400 decision stops over 20 in-game years, the agent drafts players, trades, negotiates contracts, invests in facilities and youth development, sets lineups, and manages finances under board pressure. Each stop is a fresh conversation; the only persistent memory is a self-authored notebook, so long-term memory curation is part of the task. The environment is built to instantiate four demands of management: hidden information, cumulative consequences, a counter-adaptive environment, and multi-objective pressure. Evaluation has two tracks. In the solo track, each model plays against a frozen scripted world, isolating long-horizon management without live competition. In the Arena, 15 frontier models share one persistent 20-year world and compete for the same resources. To keep this measurable, the authors use equal-endowment drafting, sealed-bid conflict resolution, and capped revival. Scoring is fully programmatic and cumulative. Beyond the final scalar score, the paper decomposes behavior into six capability dimensions to explain why models succeed or fail. The key design trade-off is deliberate: less open-ended realism in exchange for long horizon, competition, reproducibility, and objective grading all at once.1e:T644,The benchmark evaluates 15 flagship models on both solo and arena tracks, with three seeds each. All models complete the full 20-year horizon, while blind scripted anchors, including a disciplined heuristic baseline, die out in 7 of 9 runs, showing that the environment is not trivially solved by fixed rules. On performance, claude-fable-5 ranks first on both tracks. In solo, it scores about 90.94, compared with a scripted upper anchor that is allowed to read hidden state and scores 95.54, so it reaches roughly 95% of that upper bound. Competition changes the picture substantially: in Arena, league titles rotate among ten models, and mid-table solo rankings do not transfer cleanly to the shared economy. The behavioral analysis is one of the strongest parts. Reducing slow-payoff investment near the horizon correlates with final score at about Spearman -0.58; keeping cash deployed rather than idle correlates at about -0.50; opening contract renewals earlier correlates at about +0.45. These relationships hold across seeds. By contrast, token spend is uncorrelated with score under every accounting scheme. The paper also reports that hundreds of rejected bids do not reliably teach models the market’s true prices, and notebook memory fails in two opposite regimes: append-only accumulation and wholesale rewrites. A small human comparison is included: six first-time human players ran the same track, four failed to survive, and the best finished near the bottom of the model board. That mainly indicates a steep environment learning curve rather than a clean human-versus-model statement.1f:T4ea,For a quick but useful read, start with the introduction and the formal definition of management. The important point is that this is not just another game benchmark; it combines partial observability, delayed consequences, adaptive rivals, and multi-objective constraints. Then read the environment and tool sections to understand the mechanics: 26 tools, 20 years, 340 to 400 decision stops, fresh conversations at each stop, and notebook-only persistent memory. In the results, first compare the solo and arena leaderboards. That difference is central because it shows that competence in a frozen world does not transfer cleanly to a competitive shared economy. After that, spend time on the capability decomposition and correlation analysis. Those sections contain the most actionable findings, especially around slow-payoff investment, cash deployment, contract timing, and notebook failure modes. A solid skim should take around 30 minutes. If deciding whether to follow this line of work, focus on three questions: whether the scoring is fully reproducible and judge-free, whether the Arena setup isolates competition cleanly, and whether the behavioral profiles are rich enough to guide training improvements rather than just produce another ranking.20:T413,The main tool is a judge-as-latent-solver model. For a single-answer task with answer-space size k, a reference-free judge implicitly solves the problem with grading-context competence c. If it is wrong, its internal answer is assumed to be uniformly distributed over the other k-1 answers. The judge score is then whether the candidate answer matches the judge’s internal answer. Under this model, TPR is c and FPR is (1-c)/(k-1), giving AUC = 0.5 * (1 + c - (1-c)/(k-1)). This yields a necessary condition: c must exceed 1/k for the judge to discriminate above chance. The paper also shows that marginal AUC can be confounded by item difficulty; a scorer that only reads difficulty can appear useful. The proposed measurement therefore uses a within-question estimator, comparing correct and incorrect candidates for the same question, with question-clustered bootstrap uncertainty. The judge is run as a non-intervening probe: the real optimizer still accepts or rejects candidates using the verifier, while the judge only records scores.21:T426,The headline findings are straightforward and useful. First, augmentation and automation rankings are only moderately correlated overall, with a model-level rank correlation of about 0.48. Second, the correlation varies sharply by task, from -0.04 on travel planning to 0.85 on tax preparation, showing that the overlap between being a strong solver and a strong assistant is task dependent. Third, assistance is not always beneficial. The paper reports that GPT-5-mini ranks first on average in both regimes, but in augmentation the unaided worker baseline, where GPT-3.5-Turbo works without any external help, achieves the second-best average rank. That means some assistance harms performance, likely because it is too complex, poorly calibrated, or mismatched to the worker. The task-level reversals are especially informative: for example, Claude-Opus-4.8 is among the strongest direct solvers on market trends analysis with mean rank 2.05, yet among the weakest assistants on the same task with 8.15, while GPT-4.1 shows the opposite profile on counseling.22:T44d,The paper evaluates on MMAU, MMAR, MMSU, and MMAU-Pro. It reports that the alignment-only model can match or exceed more heavily post-trained open-source audio-language models while using substantially less data. On the instruction-following split of MMAU-Pro, freezing the LLM helps preserve its original instruction-following behavior. The ablations are more informative than the aggregate scores: scaling alignment data mainly helps open-ended reasoning tasks; closed-set recognition saturates quickly when the encoder lacks the needed information. Encoder sweeps show that benchmark performance tracks the capability emphasized during encoder pretraining. LLM sweeps suggest the recipe transfers across model generations, with a caveat that the generator used for synthetic responses should be well matched to the target LLM. The paper also finds that dense synthetic captions per sample are not strictly necessary; diverse open-source captions can work when aggregated. The provided content does not include full per-task numbers or dataset sizes, so the result tables should be checked directly.23:T5df,The proposed method is geometric iterative retrieval. It has three main ingredients. First, the model predicts per-layer continuous codebook vectors rather than discrete indices or a single full latent. This preserves the semantic hierarchy of RVQ while exposing the geometry of the codebook space. Second, training uses a CLIP-style symmetric contrastive objective instead of MSE. For each layer and time position, the predicted vector is trained to be closer in cosine similarity to the true codebook vector than to negatives from the batch. This addresses two failure modes at once: classification treats all wrong entries as equally wrong, while regression encourages mean-seeking behavior. Third, the method replaces the standard additive RVQ conditioning assumption with a self-attention aggregator over previously predicted lower layers, allowing non-additive cross-layer interactions. The backbone is a 12-layer bidirectional DeBERTa-v3 with hidden size 1536. During training, causal masking along the codebook axis lets the model learn all higher-layer predictions in one forward pass using ground-truth lower-layer conditioning. During inference, prediction proceeds autoregressively over RVQ layers: each continuous prediction is snapped to the nearest codebook entry and then used to condition the next layer. Compared with diffusion-based resynthesis, the key difference is that the iterative axis is aligned with the codec hierarchy itself rather than an externally imposed noise schedule.24:T401,Experiments are run on DAC codec restoration for both speech and music. The method is compared against single-pass token prediction, MSE regression, cosine regression, and related learned baselines. The main empirical result is consistent: geometric iterative retrieval achieves the best LSD among learned baselines and is preferred in double-blind human listening tests. The ablations support three claims: 1) iterative continuous prediction is better than one-shot continuous regression, so the refinement structure matters; 2) contrastive retrieval works better than direct vector regression, suggesting that codebook geometry is better exploited through retrieval-style objectives; 3) self-attention aggregation improves over simple additive conditioning, indicating that the codec's additive residual structure is not the right inductive bias for the predictor. Inference takes D-1 deterministic steps, matching standard coarse-to-fine RVQ prediction and avoiding the longer sampling chains typical of diffusion methods.25:T912,方法部分的关键不在于引入一个复杂新目标，而在于把 capability imbalance 对应到三个彼此独立的失真源，然后一一修正。

第一部分是 token-share balancing。作者观察到，按 prompt 均匀采样并不能带来按优化强度均匀训练，因为 OPD/RL 类目标是 token 级求和，长序列天然占据更多梯度份额。于是他们不再用固定 batch 内样本数控制 domain 配比，而是直接设定 weighted token-share target，让不同 domain 按目标 token 份额分配优化预算。这里的重点是“按 token share 控制”，而不是简单 oversample 短任务，因为 oversample 只在静态长度分布下近似有效，训练过程中 reward 强度变化后仍会漂移。

第二部分是 gap-aware allocation。即便初始 token 预算拉平，不同 domain 的 student-teacher gap 会以不同速度收敛。若仍固定配比，已经接近收敛的 domain 会继续消耗预算，而落后 domain 得不到补偿。作者因此用剩余 gap 来动态调度预算，把更多更新分给还没追上 teacher 的 domain。这里他们特别强调，不能只做 naive reward normalization，因为那会把预算错误地推向“看起来 reward 大但其实已接近收敛”的部分，文中给了一个在 step 74 直接训练崩掉的反例。

第三部分是 student reward refresh。标准多步 rollout reuse 会在多个 gradient step 中重复使用同一批 rollout 上基于旧 student 计算的 reward/advantage。对于依赖 student log-prob 的 OPD 信号，这会迅速变 stale，尤其在不同 domain 收敛速度不同的时候，staleness 会进一步放大预算失真。作者的做法是缓存 teacher states，但在每个 gradient step 前重新计算 student log-prob，从而刷新 student-dependent reward 组件。这样保留了 teacher 侧缓存带来的效率，又避免了 stale advantage。

全文还有一个重要的负结果：作者专门测了 token-level teacher disagreement，结论是它不像很多人预想的那样是主导瓶颈。也就是说，多 teacher 之间监督不一致并不是主要矛盾，真正的问题是训练信号如何被分配、何时失真、何时过时。这一点让方法更像“优化与预算控制”问题，而不是“多专家冲突消解”问题。26:T78a,实验设计很扎实，重点在于把 integration gap 定量化。基座是 SmolLM3-3B-Base，先做三域 mixed-SFT，再训练三个 domain RL teacher，然后用 oracle routing 做 M-OPD。这样 deployment 时的 RouteOPD/RouteRL 可以作为上界或参考，避免把路由错误混进结论里。

核心结果有几组数字需要记住。标准 naive M-OPD 在六个 benchmark 上平均分 28.05，而把每个 domain 单独蒸馏后再按路由部署可到 31.55，说明单域蒸馏本身没那么难，难的是统一到一个 student。作者进一步用 recovery 指标衡量 student 从 mixed-SFT 到 RouteRL 可获得提升里实际拿回了多少，naive M-OPD 只恢复 35.6%，Open-MOPD 提升到 83.4%。这是全文最能说明问题被真正修复的数字。

分 domain 看，instruction following 的退化最严重：相对 RouteOPD 参考低 6.16 分，且退化幅度是 math 的 3.3 倍，并且最早进入 plateau。作者给出的机制证据也很直接：IF 虽然占 20.3% 的输入 prompt，但只贡献 0.99% 的 gradient tokens。这个数字几乎把“按 prompt 平衡不等于按优化平衡”讲透了。

消融实验是文章最值得看的部分。作者分别验证了三种失真源：
1) 序列长度差异会直接造成 token budget 倾斜；
2) 即便初始 token 配平，随着 student 对不同 teacher 收敛速度不同，预算会在几十步内再次漂移；
3) rollout reuse 带来的 stale student reward 会进一步恶化这种漂移。

在方法消融上，token-share balancing、gap-aware allocation、student reward refresh 是累积增益，而不是互相替代。文中还展示了 naive reward normalization 在 step 74 崩溃，说明“简单把 reward 拉平”不是稳妥修复。作者强调他们的方法额外开销很低，尤其 refresh 只重算 student log-prob、缓存 teacher states，因此在 8×A100-80GB 的学术环境里可复现。27:T411,This is valuable because it turns an industrially relevant but poorly explained practice into a measurable optimization problem. The contribution is less about a new distillation slogan and more about a clean mechanistic decomposition: sequence-length imbalance, convergence-rate imbalance, and reward staleness. That decomposition matters beyond this paper. It informs how to think about token budget allocation, mixture weighting, and training dynamics at the pretrain/post-train interface. The work also provides an open and reproducible recipe at an academic compute scale, which is rare for RL-heavy post-training pipelines. The gain is not cosmetic: under the paper’s recovery metric, the student goes from recovering only 35.6% of the available RouteRL improvement over mixed-SFT to 83.4%. That suggests the limitation is largely in the optimization protocol, not simply teacher quality or student capacity. Anyone working on unified post-training, continual capability integration, or multi-objective RL should read this carefully.28:T819,The method is strong because it maps each observed failure mode to a specific correction instead of introducing a monolithic objective.

First, token-share balancing addresses the fact that prompt-balanced sampling does not imply optimization-balanced training. In OPD-style objectives, longer responses contribute more token-level loss and therefore more gradient mass. The authors replace implicit prompt-level balancing with an explicit weighted token-share target across domains. The point is not just to oversample short tasks. Oversampling is only a rough proxy under a fixed length distribution, while token-share control directly targets the quantity that drives optimization.

Second, gap-aware allocation handles the fact that even if raw token budgets are equalized at the start, they drift quickly because the student closes the teacher gap at different speeds in different domains. A fixed allocation then wastes updates on already-converged domains while under-training lagging ones. The proposed mechanism dynamically steers budget toward domains with the largest remaining student–teacher gap. The paper also shows why naive reward normalization is not enough and can even destabilize training by funneling budget into the wrong places.

Third, student reward refresh fixes staleness introduced by multi-step rollout reuse. In standard OPD/PPO-like training, the same rollout is reused for several gradient steps, while token-level rewards or advantages depend partly on student log-probabilities. Those student-dependent terms become stale quickly, especially when domains move at different rates. The authors cache teacher states for efficiency but recompute student log-probabilities before each gradient step, refreshing the student-side reward component at low overhead.

An important negative result is that token-level teacher disagreement does not appear to be the dominant bottleneck under their metric. That matters. It shifts the framing from “multi-teacher conflict resolution” to “optimization budget allocation and signal freshness.”29:T7c0,The experimental setup is carefully controlled. Starting from SmolLM3-3B-Base, the authors build a three-domain mixed-SFT model, train three RL teachers, and then run oracle-routed M-OPD. This gives clean references such as RouteOPD and RouteRL, so the integration gap can be measured without confounding routing errors.

The headline numbers are clear. Naive M-OPD reaches an average score of 28.05 on six benchmarks, while distilling each domain separately and deploying with routing reaches 31.55. So single-domain distillation is not the main issue; unifying capabilities in one student is. Using their recovery metric—how much of the RouteRL-over-mixed-SFT improvement is actually recovered by the unified student—naive M-OPD recovers only 35.6%, whereas Open-MOPD reaches 83.4%.

The domain breakdown is especially revealing. Instruction following suffers the largest degradation: it falls 6.16 points below its RouteOPD reference, which is 3.3 times the degradation seen in math, and it plateaus earliest during training. The mechanism-level evidence is unusually concrete: instruction-following prompts account for 20.3% of inputs but only 0.99% of gradient tokens. That single statistic captures why prompt balancing is not enough.

The ablations are the core of the paper. They show that: (1) sequence-length differences directly skew token budget, (2) even initially balanced token shares drift apart within tens of steps because domains converge at different rates, and (3) stale student-dependent rewards from rollout reuse further worsen the imbalance. On the method side, token-share balancing, gap-aware allocation, and student reward refresh provide cumulative gains rather than redundant ones. The paper also includes a useful failure case where naive reward normalization collapses training at step 74. The implementation remains practical: the full pipeline is reproducible on 8×A100-80GB, and the refresh mechanism is cheap because teacher states are cached.2a:T5d4,Read it in three passes.

First, get the evaluation frame right. Focus on the introduction and setup until RouteRL, RouteOPD, M-OPD, and the recovery metric are fully clear. Those references are essential; otherwise the later diagnosis can look like ordinary ablation work.

Second, spend time on the diagnosis section. This is where the paper earns its value. Pay attention to the evidence that catastrophic gradient conflict is not the main culprit, how prompt share diverges from gradient token share, why concise instruction-following tasks stall early, and how student–teacher gaps evolve differently across domains. The per-domain training curves and token-share statistics are more informative than the final leaderboard-style numbers.

Third, read the method section together with the corresponding ablations. Each module fixes a different distortion source: token-share balancing for length bias, gap-aware allocation for unequal convergence rates, and student reward refresh for stale rewards under rollout reuse. It helps to compare these choices against standard PPO/OPD practice, especially how advantages are computed and reused.

If time is limited, prioritize the problem-definition figures, the per-domain result tables, the instruction-following token-share analysis, the step-74 collapse example, and the cumulative ablation. A careful 60–90 minute read is enough to capture the main mechanism. The appendix is worth checking if you care about reproducing the pipeline.2b:T44e,Two pillars: controlled ablations and a supervision decomposition.
- Controlled setup: keep the student always in non-thinking mode during both training and evaluation; vary only the teacher’s generation mode (thinking vs non-thinking) and the teacher’s access to references (correct, incorrect, none, or from other problems).
- Decomposition: for trajectories generated by the student at checkpoint c, compare next-token distributions:
  T = teacher with reference, N = teacher without reference, S_c = student.
  Using log-probability vectors, decompose total teacher supervision direction:
  D_tot = (T − S_c) = D_ref + D_rec,
  where D_ref = (T − N) captures reference-induced supervision and D_rec = (N − S_c) captures “recovery” supervision independent of the reference.
- Alignment analysis: measure how much D_ref aligns with D_tot and how student prediction changes align with these directions over training.
- Controls: swap in contexts/references from other problems to test whether apparent alignment is spurious and to avoid over-attributing effects to the correct reference.2c:T5da,The method combines activation patching and attention analysis across 29 languages, five open-source model families, and six checkpoints. The experimental design is deliberately controlled. The authors build verb inventories from morphological resources such as UniMorph, then create zero-shot prompts with a common template like “Conjugation of the verb X in present tense: pronoun conjugation,” adapted to each language. This is less natural than free-form text, but it preserves token-position alignment between clean and corrupted prompts, which is important for causal patching. They distinguish two recovery metrics: a broader target-form recovery metric, which asks whether patching a head helps restore the context-appropriate target token, and a stricter minimal-pair inflection recovery metric, which isolates restoration of the inflectional contrast itself. For each language, they identify the attention heads causally implicated in agreement, then compare these head-level signatures across languages. The key claim is not just that overlap exists, but that overlap varies systematically with overt inflection. Languages with clear person/number marking show stronger circuit similarity. English behaves as a bridge case: its third-person singular cases align more with strongly inflecting languages, while its non-inflecting cases do not. The paper also examines attention patterns of overlapping heads and finds that they play similar routing roles, not merely similar locations.2d:T4f8,The study covers 29 languages, five model families, and six checkpoints. The initial lexical inventory spans 34 languages, with the number of verb lemmas per language ranging from 56 to 23,782 and averaging about 1,750. The evaluation focuses on four person/number contrasts: 1sg↔2sg, 1sg↔3sg, 1pl↔3pl, and 3sg↔3pl; English has overt inflection in only two of these. The main findings are threefold. First, languages with overt subject-verb agreement show substantial overlap in the heads implicated in agreement, especially under the stricter minimal-pair inflection metric. Second, languages with weak or absent person-based inflection are much less similar to that shared signature, which argues against a simple “all multilingual processing is shared” story. Third, model family, size, and instruction tuning affect the absolute pattern but do not overturn the main trend. The provided text does not include the full numerical tables, so exact similarity values or head counts should be taken from the original figures rather than inferred here. The most important empirical artifacts to inspect are the language-by-language similarity matrices, the comparison between broad recovery and inflection-specific recovery, and the English bridge-case breakdown.2e:T566,The analysis extends prior work on diffusion over mixture distributions by giving each cluster its own low-dimensional Gaussian structure. The first key step is to express the mixture score as a posterior-weighted average of cluster-wise scores. This turns denoising into a dynamical Bayesian classifier: at high noise, posterior mass is spread across clusters and the trajectory explores; once the signal-to-noise ratio crosses a threshold, the posterior concentrates and the trajectory commits to a single cluster with high probability. The threshold is shown to occur at SNR of order Theta(log(KD)/D). The second key step is to split the reverse process at this commitment time. Before commitment, the analysis handles inter-cluster ambiguity; after commitment, it studies denoising within one cluster and exploits its low intrinsic dimension. This yields KL error bounds whose dominant dimensional dependence is linear in the maximum intrinsic dimension across clusters, with only logarithmic dependence on D. Relative to prior single-manifold or purely qualitative mixture analyses, the novelty is the joint treatment of multimodal phase transition and intrinsic-dimension-sensitive KL bounds. The limitation is also clear: the framework relies on strong assumptions, especially Gaussian components, separation conditions, and regularity needed to control discretization error.2f:T698,DART-SD has three main components. First, it builds an Interaction-State Transition Graph from teacher rollouts. Nodes are not single actions; they are cumulative interaction states, defined by the task-relevant information acquired so far. To make this possible, the method introduces information atom abstraction: tool outputs are canonicalized, semantically equivalent responses are mapped to the same atom, and non-informative outputs such as status-only or empty responses are collapsed away. This lets trajectories with different action orders but the same acquired information land on the same node, which naturally captures the diamond topology induced by order-independent sub-goals. Second, for a failed student rollout, the method performs success-reachable projection and identifies the Critical Topological Breakpoint. Instead of cutting by timestep, it asks whether each cumulative student state still lies in the region from which success is reachable in the empirical graph. The CTB is the point where the rollout leaves that success-supported region. Third, starting from a recovery anchor near the CTB, the method retrieves a success-supported continuation from the ISTG and applies localized supervision only to the recovery segment, while preserving the valid prefix. Training is progressive self-distillation: the student rolls out, exposes its current capability boundary, receives CTB-guided local correction, and gradually extends the length of valid interaction prefixes. Compared with vanilla SFT, supervision is localized. Compared with RL, the method does not rely on sparse reward propagation; it uses explicit graph structure extracted from teacher behavior.30:T47e,The study evaluates on five benchmarks and two model scales. The main empirical claim is consistent improvement over supervised and RL baselines, along with fewer redundant tool calls and longer valid interaction prefixes. The full numerical tables are not present in the provided text, so it would be unsafe to invent exact gains, parameter counts, or data sizes. From the paper content that is available, the important experimental takeaways are: first, DART-SD helps most in tasks with multiple order-independent sub-goals, where linear imitation tends to over-penalize alternative valid paths; second, many failed student trajectories already contain long correct prefixes, so local recovery is more efficient than rewriting the whole trajectory; third, using cumulative interaction states matters, and weaker action-level graph variants lose performance; fourth, progressive self-distillation outperforms one-shot correction, suggesting that the student’s recoverable frontier expands over training. For practical use, the original tables should be checked for benchmark-specific gains, tool-call reduction, and consistency across model sizes.31:T40f,The analysis covers 1,338 public agent trajectories on PostTrainBench, spanning seven benchmarks, four base models from 1.7B to 4B parameters, and 20 agent configurations across five scaffolds. Each trajectory has a 10-hour budget on one NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU. The main finding is strategy lock-in. The high-level strategy is usually fixed at the beginning, before code and experiments provide feedback, and the rest of the run is spent on local adjustments. The pattern depends more on the agent scaffold than on the task: 80.7% of Claude Code trajectories converge on full-parameter SFT, while 89.6% of Codex CLI trajectories converge on PEFT. The experience scaffold improves execution, with reported gains of +12.6 points on GSM8K and +40.8 on HumanEval, but does not make strategies adaptive. Human review can redirect the initial strategy before training begins, yet agents still fall back to local adjustment after training starts. Spending 2–8x more inference tokens helps easier tasks but gives almost no gain on the hardest one.32:T52f,SkillGate introduces two disjoint credit channels within a single policy-gradient update. The task channel uses the usual group-normalized outcome advantage, but removes the entire skill-read tool call from its support. In other words, task success or failure no longer directly updates the tokens that name the chosen skill. The selector channel applies an action-local advantage only to the token span corresponding to the chosen skill name. That selector signal is positive only when the trajectory issued a single read and the selected skill matches the oracle-correct one; it is then centered within the prompt group to reduce between-group difficulty effects. Two implementation details matter. First, rollout-time span attribution records the exact token span of each skill-read action so the support partition is exact in practice. Second, length-invariant reweighting gives equal batch-level loss weight to the two channels and equal share to each read action, preventing selector learning from weakening on longer trajectories. This differs from finer reward shaping: the paper is not slicing the same reward more finely, but separating two decisions that are justified by different evidence. It also differs from pre-execution retrieval methods, because the target here is the policy’s own in-episode skill choice.33:T4ac,The paper first audits the failure mode, then validates the method. In the audit, it analyzes 12,800 on-policy trajectories from a standard outcome-only RL run and reports three key findings. First, share: the median token share of the selected-skill name is only 0.14% of a trajectory, and this shrinks further with horizon. Second, sign: about two in five of those tokens receive negative advantage; on the longest trajectories, a correct choice is punished more often than rewarded. Third, value: within matched prompt groups, making the correct read is still worth +11.2 points in task success, so the decision is important despite receiving weak and noisy credit. The method is then evaluated on five agent benchmarks. The headline number in the provided text is trial success improving from 47.0% to 53.2%. The gains appear concentrated in long-horizon settings with similar-looking candidate skills and noisy downstream execution, which matches the proposed diagnosis. The most important ablations are likely the ones isolating support partitioning, local selector advantage, and length-invariant weighting. Previous works formulate the edge of stability with respect to the maximum Hessian eigenvalue and the learning rate. However, we observe that many first-order methods, including gradient descent, significantly violate the stability bound predicted by these theories by a fact","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-19T01:03:34Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2608_18415.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"深度学习优化中的“稳定边缘”（Edge of Stability, EoS）现象表明，梯度下降可以在经典理论预测为不稳定的区域（Hessian 最大特征值超过阈值）稳定运行。然而，现有理论基于最大 Hessian 特征值和学习率的公式，无法解释不同优化器（如 AdaGrad）在实际训练中大幅偏离理论稳定阈值（高达 21.1 倍）的系统性偏差。","importance":"挑战了现有的 EoS 理论框架，揭示了优化器在 EoS 现象中的主动作用。提出的“实现稳定边缘”（Realized Edge of Stability）新公式，不仅消除了优化器依赖的偏差，还为理解复杂优化器（如自适应优化器）在非凸损失景观中的行为提供了更精确的诊断工具。","technical_approach":"指出传统 EoS 公式假设优化器总是能“实现”（realize）最坏模式的锐度（sharpness），但这在实际中并不成立。作者从优化器操作的实际动力系统出发，结合方向 Hessian（directional Hessian）和梯度-更新对齐度（gradient-update alignment），推导出一个新的稳定阈值公式 $\\zeta$。该公式将实际的 EoS 分解为三个可解释的因子：原始 EoS $\\chi$、空间参与度（spatial participation）$\\varsigma$ 和时间校准（temporal calibration）$\\tau$。","experiments":"在多种优化器（GD、动量优化器、AdaGrad 等）和不同学习率下进行了广泛实验。结果表明，传统 EoS 阈值 $\\chi$ 的实际值在 0.79 到 21.07 之间波动，严重偏离理论值 1。而新提出的实现 EoS $\\zeta$ 成功消除了这种优化器依赖的偏移，使其值稳定在理论预测附近。实验还展示了不同优化器如何通过调整空间和时间因子来“驾驶”在稳定边缘上。","reading_guide":"精读 Introduction 中对现有 EoS 理论局限性的分析，以及 Figure 1 中展示的惊人偏差。重点理解新公式 $\\zeta$ 的推导逻辑，特别是方向 Hessian 和梯度-更新对齐度的引入。实验部分关注不同优化器在 $\\zeta$、$\\varsigma$ 和 $\\tau$ 上的差异，这有助于深入理解自适应优化器为何能在高学习率下保持稳定。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The 'Edge of Stability' (EoS) phenomenon in deep learning optimization shows that gradient descent can operate stably in regions where classical theory predicts instability (i.e., the maximum Hessian eigenvalue exceeds a threshold). However, existing theories based on the maximum Hessian eigenvalue and learning rate fail to explain the systematic deviations—up to a factor of 21.1—observed with different optimizers (e.g., AdaGrad) from the theoretical stability threshold.","importance":"Challenges the existing EoS theoretical framework and reveals the active role of optimizers in the EoS phenomenon. Starting from the actual dynamical system the optimizer operates on, and incorporating the directional Hessian and gradient-update alignment, the authors derive a new stability threshold formulation, $\\zeta$. This formulation factorizes the realized EoS into three interpretable components: the original EoS $\\chi$, spatial participation $\\varsigma$, and temporal calibration $\\tau$.","experiments":"Extensive experiments were conducted across various optimizers (GD, momentum-based, AdaGrad, etc.) and learning rates. This gradient-objective consistency allows a curious server to recover private labels by searching for a sequence whose induced gradient explains the observation. We propose Gradient Mirage, a defense that breaks this consistency without discarding the optimization utility o","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"glance","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-19T10:20:33Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2608_18767.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"研究 LLM split learning 中服务端能否通过接口梯度恢复客户端私有标签，并提出在保持可训练性的同时降低标签可识别性的防御。","importance":"贡献类型是训练系统安全方法。它关注分布式或隐私保护 LLM 训练中的梯度泄露问题，对 split/federated training 场景有参考价值；但不是通用预训练 recipe，影响范围取决于是否采用 split learning 架构。","technical_approach":"提出 Gradient Mirage，核心是打破暴露梯度与客户端完整标签训练目标之间的一致性，使攻击者求解一个 misspecified inverse problem。具体包括三类不一致：objective、direction 和 scale。Selective Autoregressive Supervision 用 masked surrogate loss 产生暴露梯度，而非攻击者假设的完整标签目标；Scale Blinding 等机制进一步扰动梯度尺度和方向，同时尽量保留反向信号的优化效用。","experiments":"摘要未给出具体模型参数量、数据集、攻击成功率下降或训练性能保持数字。实验应关注对 gradient matching attacks 的 label recovery 降低幅度，以及下游训练 loss、accuracy 或 perplexity 的退化程度。","authors_note":"作者来自 Nanjing University、Hohai University 和 Nanjing Normal University。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies whether a server in LLM split learning can recover private client labels from interface gradients, and proposes a defense that keeps gradients useful for training while making labels unidentifiable.","importance":"This is a training-system security contribution. con","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-19T03:24:51Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_18486.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 保留标准 decoder block，但替换每层独立的 KV 生成方式。对每个 token，模型收集 L 层 hidden states，先做 RMSNorm，让不同层处在相近尺度；然后由 router 为 K 分支和 V 分支分别产生 k×L 的混合权重。每个 KV channel 是所有源层状态的内容相关加权混合，再经过共享的 K/V projection 得到可缓存的 key 和 value。每个 consumer layer 固定选择一个 channel，因此不同层可以读取不同的跨层混合表示。这个设计同时具备三点：过去 token 的深层状态可以被当前浅层读取，连接模式对不同 consumer layer 不同，混合权重还依赖源 token 内容。难点在训练和 prefill：每个 token 的 KV 依赖该 token 完成后的全层状态，而这些状态又依赖之前 each layer attends to past tokens only through KV states produced at the same depth. Deeper representations of past tokens have already been computed, but shallow layers of later tokens cannot directly use them. WhiteMatter introduces content-dependent all-to-all cross-layer access to past-token representations through a shared KV-channel mechanism.","importance":"This is an architecture paper with direct relevance to pretraining. It changes the information flow between depth and time: computation performed at deeper layers for earlier tokens becomes available to later tokens, including their shallow layers. The design also connects cross-layer feedback with KV-cache control, since using k channels for L layers reduces the KV cache to k/L of the standard size when k < L. The evidence is based on 8B-token training runs, so the result is useful but still early for large-scale LLM settings.","technical_approach":"WhiteMatter keeps the standard decoder blocks but replaces per-layer KV production with a cross-layer KV pool. For each token, the model gathers hidden states from all L layers, RMS-normalizes them, and uses a router to produce k-by-L mixing weights. Separate routers or parameters are used for keys and values. Each KV channel is a content-dependent signed mixture of all source-layer states, followed by shared K and V projections. Each consumer layer reads a fixed channel, allowing different layers to receive different mixtures of source depths. This gives the model deep-to-shallow feedback across tokens, consumer-specific connectivity, and token-dependent routing. The main training complication is circular dependency: a token's KV depends on its completed layer states, while those states depend on previous tokens' KV. The paper uses a cyclic Gauss-Seidel schedule to solve this iteratively while retaining token-parallel training and prefill, with faster convergence than Jacobi-style iteration.","experiments":"All models are pretrained from scratch on 8B tokens of FineWeb-Edu under matched data, token budget, and optimizer settings. It also slightly beats a 24-layer vanilla model at 20.181 perplexity. With half cache, k=8, WhiteMatter reaches 20.377 perplexity and is 5.0% better than an LCKV baseline with the same cache size. The 16-layer variants also outperform other 16-layer models on LAMBADA and WikiText. In a controlled exact-autoregressive setting, cyclic Gauss-Seidel with g=16 comes within 1% of autoregressive perplexity in 4 passes. Converged prefill is 13.9x faster than exact autoregressive evaluation and 11.2x faster than Jacobi iteration. The reported cost is that cyclic training for the 16-layer experiments is about 1.5x more expensive than vanilla training.","reading_guide":"Plan for a 30–40 minute skim. Instead of dropping tokens entirely, it compresses the value vectors of retained tokens. Specifically: 1) Value vectors are transformed into an orthogonal space and partitioned into groups; 2) A lightweight role estimator generates a soft latent representation based on the token representation and language context; 3) This latent representation dynamically determines which value groups to retain. This routing structure is trained jointly with the policy network, allowing the model to discover feature dimensions useful for action prediction.","experiments":"Evaluated on OpenVLA-OFT-7B and the LIBERO benchmark. Asking a model for operators directly does not work: 183","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-19T09:55:17Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_18744.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.SE"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文研究开放式生成任务中自动评测指标如何构造，重点是没有参考答案、LLM judge 又有偏置时，能否让指标通过自身误判逐步改进。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接关系不在训练目标本身，而在评测信号构造。它提供了一种可审计、可执行、可组合的自动评测生成方法，适合思考开放式任务、agent 训练、报告生成、代码生成等场景中的 reward 或 verifier 如何从局部缺陷检测开始建立。对基座研究员的启发是：复杂质量判断不一定要先学一个整体打分器，也可以拆成一组小的、可验证的 defect operator，再用反例驱动覆盖盲点。","technical_approach":"方法叫 EvalCEGAR，借鉴程序验证里的 counterexample-guided abstraction refinement。系统维护一个 Python operator 池，每个 operator 读取任务和候选答案，判断是否存在某个命名缺陷，或者选择 abstain。operator 池把一个候选答案映射成一串 verdict signature；如果两个答案得到相同 signature，但真实标签不同，就说明当前指标无法区分这对样本。系统把这对 collision 作为新的规格说明交给模型，让模型生成能区分它们的 operator。这样做的关键区别是：不是反复要求模型“写一个评测器”，而是用当前指标的失败样例限定下一次生成目标。论文还强调接口问题：如果现有 operator 可读信息不足以表达某个区分，继续采样没有意义，需要扩大 operator 的输入接口。operator 是否纳入池中，不看它覆盖多少已知错误，而看它是否改善下游二分类决策。","experiments":"实验选择代码任务，因为隐藏测试可以提供精确 oracle，但 operator 本身不能读取 oracle。数据来自 MBPP+ 和 HumanEval+。作者抽取 150 个 MBPP+ 任务，其中 75 个用于运行循环、75 个保留；再加入 228 个 MBPP+ 任务和 125 个 HumanEval+ 任务，形成 428 个 unseen tasks。直接让模型生成 operator 的朴素循环表现很差：5 次运行产生 183 个会触发 flag 的 operator，但只对应 96 种不同 flag set，行为重复明显；对研究中写出的 295 个 operator 重新打分也没有带来发现。EvalCEGAR 的 8 次运行中有 6 次成功纳入 operator，且这 6 个 operator 在 428 个未见任务上都有帮助。最好的自动生成 operator 只有 55 行 Python，在第二个 benchmark 上达到最佳手写 operator 的效果，但触发 flag 数只有后者约三分之一。消融显示，单独改变 admission question 可把 admission 从 0 提到 1；窄接口设置下 336 次尝试没有纳入任何 operator。组合实验枚举了约 230 万个可组合子集，发现原循环使用的组合目标在全集中排名可低至第 5 百分位，而一个未调参的 class-weighted 目标可达到第 91 百分位以上，说明 operator 生成和 operator 组合是两个需要分开处理的问题。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 Introduction、Section 4、Section 5.1–5.3，约 25–35 分钟。重点看三个问题：第一，signature collision 如何定义指标盲点；第二，operator admission 为什么绑定下游决策，而不是绑定覆盖率；第三，Section 5.3 的组合枚举结果，它提醒读者不要把“发现有用局部检查器”和“构造好整体指标”混为一谈。Appendix A 和 B 可快速查看，主要用于确认 oracle 隔离和少量训练任务是否足够支持迁移。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies how to build automatic metrics for open-ended outputs when there is no reference answer and LLM judges are unreliable or biased.","importance":"Its main relevance to foundation-model research is evaluation signal construction, rather than pretraining itself. The work suggests a concrete way to turn vague quality judgments into a pool of small, executable, auditable defect detectors. This is useful for thinking about reward models, verifiers, agent training signals, and open-ended generation evaluation, especially in settings where a scalar judge is hard to trust.","technical_approach":"EvalCEGAR adapts counterexample-guided abstraction refinement from program verification. The metric is represented as a pool of Python operators. Each operator reads the task and one candidate answer, then flags one named defect, passes the answer, or abstains. The pool maps each answer to a verdict signature. If two answers share the same signature but have different oracle labels, the current metric is too coarse. That collision becomes the next specification given to the model. This is the main departure from simply prompting a model to write evaluators: diversity comes from failures of the current abstraction, not from resampling. The method also treats interface width as a first-class issue. If an operator cannot see the information needed to distinguish a pair, further sampling is not expected to help; the operator interface must be expanded. Operators are admitted only when they improve the deployed decision rule.","experiments":"$18","reading_guide":"Skim the Introduction, Section 4, and Sections 5.1–5.3. Expected reading time is about 25–35 minutes. Appendix A and Appendix B are worth a quick pass to check oracle isolation and the small amount of supervision used to seed the loop.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得扫方法循环；把 evaluator 当可调程序而非 judge prompt，这对 RL/数据筛选信号质量更有用。","en":"Skim the refinement loop; treating evaluators as editable programs rather than judge prompts is the useful signal for RL and data filtering."},"metadata":{"institutions":["AWS Generative AI Innovation Center","Affiliation:[3pt]\nAWS Generative AI Innovation Center"],"authors":["Xing Zhang","Yanwei Cui","Guanghui Wang","Zhihao Lin","Peiyang He"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["evaluation","automatic-metrics","CEGAR","agent-eval","verifier"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18744","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.18744","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.18303","title":"SESSE: Sketch, Expand, Sort, Summarize, Evaluate -- LLM-as-Judge Evaluation via Structured Decomposition","abstract":"LLM-as-judge evaluation reduces response quality assessment to a single holistic A/B preference choice, providing no mechanism to isolate which quality dimensions drove the preference or distinguish model errors from genuine label ambiguity. We propose SESSE (Sketch, Expand, Sort, Summarize, Evaluate), a training-free framework that decomposes holistic judgment into structured sub-questions mined directly from the judge's own error cases; requiring no oracle responses, task-specific rubrics, or ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-18T20:27:56Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_18303.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作解决 LLM-as-judge 的可解释性和可靠性问题。常见做法让模型直接给出 A/B 偏好，即使加上 chain-of-thought，也只是一次性的自由文本判断，很难知道偏好来自事实性、完整性、安全性、指令遵循还是其他因素，也难以区分模型误判和样本本身有歧义。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值主要在评测信号质量。偏好数据、reward model、RLHF/RLAIF 和自动评测都依赖 judge。如果 judge 只输出一个整体偏好，错误会被压缩成不可诊断的标签噪声。SESSE 提供了一种训练免费、可审计的分解方式：从 judge 自己的错误中挖掘判别标准，再把整体偏好拆成多个 A/B/NA 子问题。这对构造更稳定的偏好数据、分析 reward hacking、筛查自动标注噪声都有参考意义。","technical_approach":"SESSE 是五阶段流程。Stage 0 先用 judge 做一次整体 CoT 判断，在 dev 集上收集与标签不一致的错误样本。Stage 1 让同一个 judge 从这些错误中生成条件化评价子问题，形式是“如果某条件适用，哪个回答更满足某属性：A、B 或 NA”。这些 criterion 不依赖人工 rubric，也不需要 oracle response。Stage 2 对候选子问题做 embedding 并聚成 k 个主题簇。Stage 3 用 greedy farthest-first traversal 对每簇内子问题排序，形成可按深度截断的 active bank。Stage 4 用 LLM 为每个簇生成抽象 protasis，并用二元等价判断合并近重复簇。Stage 5 在推理时先判断哪些簇适用于当前样本，再对选中的具体子问题分别投 A/B/NA 票；NA 不计入最终 A/B tally，A/B 多数票给出最终偏好，平票则声明 tie 并从 accuracy 分母中排除。方法的关键区别是：标准来自 judge 自己的错误分布，而不是人工预设或微调学习。","experiments":"实验在 RewardBench 上进行，共 2,985 个 pairwise preference 样本，随机划分为 1,985 dev 和 1,000 held-out val。配置为 k=25 个簇，n=10，temperature=0，并在 dev 上选择各模型的最优深度 n*。评测模型包括 Qwen2-VL-7B、Gemini Flash Lite 和 Gemini 2.5 Flash。Gemini 2.5 Flash 上，SESSE 在非 tie 样本上达到 93.3% accuracy，tie rate 为 3.5%，与 RISE-Judge-32B 的 92.7% 处于相近区间；RISE-Judge 需要 SFT+DPO，而 SESSE 不更新参数。相对 CoT holistic baseline，Gemini 2.5 Flash 的差距为 1.3%，统计上不显著；Flash Lite 和 Qwen2-VL-7B 分别有 6.8% 和 5.0% 的相对差距，且 p<0.05。需要注意，SESSE accuracy 只在非 tie 样本上报告，因此读结果时要同时看 tie rate。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20–30 分钟。优先看 Method 的五阶段流程，尤其是 Stage 1 如何从错误样本生成 criterion，以及 Stage 5 如何用 A/B/NA 投票聚合。实验部分重点看 Table 1：它同时给出非 tie accuracy、tie rate 和与 holistic CoT 的差距。附录中的 depth ablation 可以帮助判断 criterion bank 的规模是否只是调参收益。若关注 reward model 或偏好数据构造，值得细看错误样本驱动的 criterion mining，因为它提供了一种把 judge failure 转成可复用评测维度的办法。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses the reliability and interpretability problem in LLM-as-judge evaluation. Standard judge setups reduce response quality to a single A/B preference. Even with chain-of-thought, the reasoning is a one-shot free-form explanation, so it is hard to identify whether the preference came from factuality, completeness, safety, instruction following, or some other dimension. It is also hard to separate model mistakes from genuinely ambiguous labels.","importance":"The main relevance to pretraining and post-training is evaluation-signal quality. Preference data, reward models, RLHF/RLAIF pipelines, and automatic model comparisons all depend on judges. A single holistic label compresses many possible failure modes into opaque noise. SESSE offers a training-free way to decompose that signal: mine criteria from the judge’s own errors, then turn a holistic preference into multiple auditable A/B/NA decisions. This is useful for diagnosing preference-label noise, studying reward hacking, and building more inspectable synthetic supervision.","technical_approach":"$19","experiments":"Experiments use RewardBench with 2,985 pairwise preference examples, split into 1,985 development examples and 1,000 held-out validation examples. The main configuration uses k=25 clusters, n=10, temperature 0, and selects each model’s optimal depth n* on the development set. Judges include Qwen2-VL-7B, Gemini Flash Lite, and Gemini 2.5 Flash. With Gemini 2.5 Flash, SESSE reaches 93.3% accuracy on non-tie validation examples with a 3.5% tie rate, close to RISE-Judge-32B at 92.7%, while RISE-Judge requires SFT+DPO and SESSE is training-free. Compared with the holistic CoT baseline, Gemini 2.5 Flash shows a 1.3% relative gap that is not statistically significant; Flash Lite and Qwen2-VL-7B show larger relative gaps of 6.8% and 5.0%, both significant at p<0.05. The non-tie reporting convention matters: SESSE accuracy should be read together with tie rate.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Focus first on the five-stage Method, especially Stage 1 criterion generation from error cases and Stage 5 A/B/NA aggregation. In the experiments, Table 1 is the key artifact because it reports non-tie accuracy, tie rate, and the gap to holistic CoT. The depth ablations in the appendix are useful for judging whether the criterion bank is robust or mainly tuned to the dev set. We evaluate whether coding agents that repair repository-level issues remain reliable when the surrounding codebase is rewritten into a semantically equivalent form. We introduce a random variant sampler that applies common semantics-preserving transformations (SPTs) - spanning control-flow rewrites, dead-code injection, and identifier renam","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-18T23:46:11Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_18389.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“代码 agent 在 SWE-bench 上能做对多少题”，而是更接近部署的问题：当代码仓库只发生语义不变、但表面形式改变的重写时，repository-level 代码 agent 的修复能力是否稳定。难点有两个。第一，agent 不是单轮补全，而是多轮搜索、编辑、执行工具的闭环系统，脆弱性可能来自模型、scaffold、工具调用策略三者的耦合。第二，LLM agent 本身有较强随机性，单次成功或失败不能直接归因于 perturbation，因此需要把“扰动效应”和“运行方差”分开估计。论文围绕这两个点，构造了一个非对抗、随机采样的 semantics-preserving transformation 评测框架，试图给出代码 agent 鲁棒性的保守下界。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在于提出新训练方法，而在于把一个常被 benchmark 平均分掩盖的问题量化了：模型可能学到的是对代码表层模式的依赖，而不是稳定的程序语义表征。这个结论对 code pretraining、代码 tokenizer、repo-level agent scaffold 设计都有启发。更具体地说，论文显示鲁棒性不是模型的单独属性，而是 model × scaffold × workload 的联合属性；高基线能力并不自动带来更强鲁棒性；只看最终 solve rate 也会低估问题，因为 step 数和 token 成本会先恶化。这些观察会影响后续两类工作：一类是代码预训练中是否要显式加入语义等价变换、重构风格多样性和 repo-level augmentation；另一类是 agent 评测是否必须做 paired robustness evaluation，而不能只报单点 leaderboard。","technical_approach":"方法上有三层设计。第一层是变换库。作者实现了 14 类 semantics-preserving transformations，覆盖控制流改写、死代码注入、标识符重命名等，既包含开发者日常可能做的 refactoring，也包含更偏 stress-test 的局部改写。语义保持的操作化定义不是形式化程序等价，而是项目测试集上的 per-test outcome 等价；这在工程上可执行，也贴近 SWE-bench 的验收方式。第二层是随机 variant sampler。它不是 adversarial search，不根据模型反馈去找最坏样本，而是随机决定对哪些位置施加哪些 SPT，并独立采样多个变体。这样做的意义是把结论解释为“简单局部、非反馈式改写已经足以造成退化”，因此得到的是影响下界，而不是上界。第三层是统计设计。由于 agent run 有随机性，作者对 unperturbed seed 和 perturbed variants 都做重复运行，并采用配对比较来隔离 perturbation effect；同时除了 issue resolve rate，还记录 step count 和 token cost，避免只看 pass/fail。实验覆盖两个 scaffold：mini-SWE agent 与 OpenCode；四个底座模型：Claude Opus 4.5、Kimi K2.5、MiniMax M2.5、Qwen 3.6-27B；任务来自 SWE-bench Verified 与 SWE-bench Pro 的 54 个实例。这里最有意思的点不是变换本身，而是评测设计：作者把鲁棒性从“单次跑分”改成“在语义等价邻域中的性能分布”，这比传统 benchmark 更接近部署风险。","experiments":"主要结果有几条。其一，简单、局部、非对抗的 SPT 就能让大多数配置出现小到中等退化，最受影响的配置 resolve rate 下降可达 6.7 个百分点，并且 16 个 model-scaffold-benchmark 配置中有 6 个出现统计显著退化。其二，即便最终 solve rate 变化不大，agent 也往往要付出更多搜索成本：step count 最多增加 9.9%，token cost 最多增加 22.9%。这说明“结果没掉太多”不等于“系统没受影响”。其三，模型能力与鲁棒性并不单调对应。在同一 scaffold 内，未扰动条件下能力更强的模型有时反而更脆；而跨 scaffold 比较时，更简单的 scaffold 往往更稳。其四，鲁棒性排序会随 scaffold 和 benchmark 改变。文中举的例子是 Qwen 在 mini-SWE agent + SWE-bench Verified 下属于较稳的一档，但在 OpenCode 下却变成最脆的一档。其五，扰动影响高度集中在少数 repository/instance 上，而不是均匀分布，这提示后续分析应关注 repo 结构、测试覆盖、命名风格、上下文检索路径等条件变量。论文的实验规模不算大，54 个实例也不足以给出行业级结论，但 paired repeated-run 设计让这些数字比单次 leaderboard 更可信。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction，抓住作者要解决的是“agent robustness under semantics-preserving rewrites”，不是一般的 code robustness。然后看 SPT 一节和表 1，理解 14 类变换覆盖了哪些代码表层因素。接着重点看实验设计部分，尤其是如何处理 agent stochasticity、为什么要做 paired runs、为什么选择非对抗随机采样而不是 adversarial attack。结果部分建议优先看三类图表：一类是 resolve rate degradation，确认哪些配置显著下降；一类是 step/token 成本变化，理解隐藏代价；一类是按 scaffold、benchmark、repository 分解的结果，体会“jagged frontier”不是修辞，而是排序不稳定。预计 25 到 35 分钟可以抓住主结论。如果要进一步深挖，值得问两个问题：一是这些脆弱性更多来自底座模型的代码表征，还是来自 scaffold 的搜索/上下文管理；二是若把 SPT augmentation 放进 code pretraining 或 agent training，是否能同时提升 solve rate 与鲁棒性，还是会出现 trade-off。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a deployment-facing question rather than a leaderboard question: if a repository is rewritten into a semantically equivalent form, do repository-level coding agents remain reliable? This is harder than standard code-LLM robustness because the system is multi-turn and agentic. Failures can come from the base model, the scaffold, or their interaction with tools and search. A second challenge is stochasticity: one failed run on a perturbed repo does not prove the perturbation caused it. The paper therefore builds a non-adversarial robustness evaluation around semantics-preserving transformations and repeated paired runs, aiming to estimate a conservative lower bound on robustness loss.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is indirect but real. The work quantifies a gap that standard coding benchmarks hide: strong performance may still rely on shallow syntactic regularities rather than stable semantic representations of code. That matters for code pretraining, tokenizer design, augmentation strategy, and repo-level agent training. The main lesson is that robustness is not a property of the model alone. It is a joint property of model, scaffold, and workload. Another useful point is that outcome-only metrics understate the damage: step count and token cost often degrade before solve rate moves much. For future work, this argues for robustness-aware code pretraining and for paired semantic-invariance evaluation instead of single-point leaderboard reporting.","technical_approach":"$1a","experiments":"$1b","reading_guide":"$1c","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"揭示了当前 Code Agent 对代码表面变化（如重命名、死代码注入）的脆弱性，说明模型对代码语义的理解仍停留在 pattern matching 层面，对构建更鲁棒的 Code Agent 评测基准有参考价值。","en":"Reveals the vulnerability of current Code Agents to superficial code changes (e.g., renaming, dead code injection), indicating that models' understanding of code semantics remains at the pattern matching level; valuable for building more robust Code Agent benchmarks."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Colorado State University","University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign","Carnegie Mellon University"],"authors":["Hasan Najib Mahmud","Shreya Gupta","Isha Chaudhary","Nathaniel Enis","Ravi Mangal","Gagandeep Singh","Corina Pasareanu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Code Agents","Robustness Evaluation","Semantics-Preserving Transformations","SWE-bench"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18389","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.18389","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.18423","title":"FM-Bench: A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Management with Competing Agents","abstract":"Language model agents now execute bounded tasks reliably. Whether they can sustain effective decision-making over long horizons, where actions have cumulative consequences and the environment responds to their choices, remains largely unmeasured. FM-Bench (Football Management Benchmark) measures this. An LLM agent runs a football club for 20 in-game years through 26 tools and roughly 340 to 400 decision stops. It drafts a squad on the same budget as every rival, trades players, negotiates contra","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-19T01:33:36Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_18423.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的是 agent 评测里的一个空缺：现有 benchmark 往往只覆盖长时程，或者只覆盖竞争环境，但很少同时考察“长期决策 + 多智能体竞争 + 累积后果 + 资源约束”。作者把这个问题具体化为 management：让一个 LLM agent 在信息不完全、对手会适应、早期决策会改变后续世界状态、且成绩与财务要同时达标的环境里，持续经营一个组织。论文用 20 个游戏年、约 340 到 400 个决策停点的足球俱乐部经营环境来承载这个定义，并试图用完全确定性的评分机制替代 LLM judge 或人工打分。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接方法贡献，更像是 agent/generalist model 的评测基础设施。但对基座研究仍有两层启发。第一，很多当前模型在短程工具使用、局部规划、单回合 reasoning 上已经不差，真正暴露能力边界的是长期信用分配、记忆压缩、策略更新和对竞争环境的适应，这些能力与预训练阶段是否学到稳定的世界模型、时间延迟回报表征、以及可压缩的状态摘要能力有关。第二，论文把“token 花得多不代表做得好”“notebook 记忆会在两种极端下失效”“被拒绝的市场反馈并不会自动转化为价格学习”这些现象量化出来，对 post-train agent 设计和未来 pretrain 数据构造都有参考价值。它更像一个高质量 stress test，而不是直接告诉你怎么训练。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 benchmark 设计，而不是新 agent。FM-Bench 构造了一个确定性的足球管理模拟器，agent 通过 26 个 schema-generated tools 在每个决策停点做操作，包括选秀、交易、续约、设施投资、青训投入、排兵布阵等。整个赛程持续 20 个游戏年，累计约 340 到 400 个 decision stops，每个 stop 都是一次新的对话，唯一可跨轮携带的状态是 agent 自己维护的 notebook，因此 benchmark 顺带测了长期记忆压缩与状态管理能力。环境设计刻意包含四个管理难点：隐藏信息，球员真实能力不可直接见；累积后果，设施和青训等投资要多年后才回报；对手适应，市场和竞争者会响应你的行为；多目标压力，成绩与财务纪律共同决定生存和得分。评测分成两个 track。Solo track 中，每个模型面对冻结的 scripted world，用来测在固定环境中的长期经营能力。Arena track 中，15 个前沿模型被放进同一个共享 20 年世界，争夺同一批资源，作者通过 equal-endowment draft、sealed-bid conflict resolution 和 capped revival 保证竞争评测可比。评分完全由环境机制累计产生，没有 LLM judge 或人工主观打分。除了最终标量分数，作者还把行为拆成六类 capability profile，用来解释模型为什么赢或输。这种设计的关键取舍是：牺牲开放世界的自然性，换取长期、竞争、可重复、可量化四件事同时成立。","experiments":"实验覆盖 15 个旗舰模型，并在 solo 与 arena 两个 track 上各跑 3 个 seed。所有模型都能跑完整个 20 年 horizon，而盲脚本基线中包括一个纪律性较强的 heuristic 在内，9 次运行里有 7 次中途“死亡”，说明环境不是靠简单规则就能稳定过关。成绩上，claude-fable-5 在两个 track 都排第一，solo 分数约 90.94，对比允许读取隐藏状态的 scripted upper anchor 95.54，接近其 95% 水平。Arena 中竞争会显著改写排名，联赛冠军在 10 个模型之间轮换，中游模型在 solo 的相对位置并不能稳定迁移到共享经济环境。作者还做了行为相关性分析：临近终局时减少慢回报投资与最终得分显著负相关，Spearman 约 -0.58；保持现金持续投入而不是闲置，相关性约 -0.50；更早开启合同续约，相关性约 +0.45。这些指标在每个 seed 上都能跟踪最终分数。相反，token spend 在不同核算方式下都与成绩不相关，说明“多想多花 token”不是长期管理能力的代理变量。另一个有意思的发现是，市场里数百次被拒绝的报价并不会让模型稳定学到真实价格带；notebook 也会在两种相反策略下失效：一种是只追加不整理，导致信息淤积；另一种是频繁整体重写，导致关键历史丢失。论文还让 6 位首次上手的人类参与同一 track，4 位中途失败，最好成绩也在模型榜单底部，这更多说明环境门槛高、学习曲线陡，而不是简单的人机优劣比较。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和任务定义，确认作者说的 management 不是一般的 game benchmark，而是四个约束同时存在的长期组织经营。然后看环境与工具设计，理解 26 个工具、20 年 horizon、340 到 400 个 decision stops、每轮新对话加 notebook 记忆这一套机制。结果部分建议先看两个 leaderboard：solo 和 arena 的差异能直接说明“固定环境能力”与“竞争环境能力”不是一回事。接着看 capability decomposition 和相关性分析，那部分比总分更有信息量，尤其是慢回报投资、现金部署、续约时机、notebook 失败模式这几项。预计 30 evaluation. Existing benchmarks usually test either long horizons or competitive interaction, but rarely both together under cumulative consequences and resource constraints. The authors define this missing setting as management: running an organization over a long horizon in a partially observed world, against adaptive rivals, where early decisions reshape later opportunities and performance is judged jointly on results and financial discipline. They instantiate this with a 20-year football-club management environment and a fully deterministic scoring pipeline, avoiding LLM judges and human raters.","importance":"This is not a direct pretraining method paper, but it matters as infrastructure for studying generalist agents and the limits of current foundation models. Existing gates rely on verifiable rewards, confining these methods to tasks with an automatic verifier. We ask a prior question: can we tell, before placing a judge in the loop, whether its scores separate correct f","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-19T09:19:23Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_18719.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文问的是：在 text-space skill optimization 中，如果没有 verifier，能否用 reference-free LLM judge 作为候选 skill 的接受门控；如果能，部署前应怎样判断这个 judge gate 是否可靠。论文把问题从“judge 准不准”改成“judge 是否能区分 verifier 标记的正确输出和错误输出”。","importance":"这篇论文和基座预训练的距离不近，但对训练信号质量有清晰启发。很多后训练流程会用 LLM judge 做数据筛选、reward、拒绝采样或自动评测。论文指出，benchmark accuracy 往往高估 judge 在实际 gating 场景里的有效能力；真正相关的是 judge 在当前任务、当前输入分布、当前候选答案上的 discriminability。这个观点可以迁移到 SFT/RL 数据过滤、reward model 审计和 synthetic data 质量控制。","technical_approach":"方法由一个简单但有用的 judge-as-latent-solver 模型开始。对于答案空间大小为 k 的单答案任务，judge 在没有 gold answer 的情况下先隐式求解，求对的概率记为 competence c；如果求错，则在其余 k-1 个答案上均匀分布；judge 的评分由候选答案是否等于其内部答案决定。在这些假设下，二值 judge score 的 TPR 为 c，FPR 为 (1-c)/(k-1)，因此 AUC = 1/2 * (1 + c - (1-c)/(k-1))。这给出一个必要条件：只有 c > 1/k 时，judge 才可能高于随机区分。论文进一步指出 marginal AUC 会被题目难度混淆：一个只读出题目难度、不看答案内容的打分器也可能得到高于随机的 AUC。因此它提出 within-question estimator，用同一问题内的正确和错误候选做比较，并用 question-clustered bootstrap 给不确定性估计。测量流程是 non-intervening probe：真实优化仍由 verifier gate 决策，judge 只旁路记录分数，不改变 accept/reject，从而避免 judge 本身改变优化动态。","experiments":"实验发现 judge discriminability 随任务变化很大。在研究数学任务上接近随机；在事实问答和研究生级科学题上较可用。论文还检验了 competence c 与 AUC 的闭式关系：在难度混淆较少、可干净测试的设置中，预测边界能解释观测结果。另一个重要发现是，headline benchmark accuracy 是偏乐观的 competence 代理。差距来自至少三类因素：censoring convention、benchmark exposure、以及 solve/grade context gap。闭环 gating 实验显示，未通过诊断的 judge 会引入 regression；通过诊断的 judge 没有引入 regression，但会出现 over-rejection，即过度拒绝本可接受的候选。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 25 到 35 分钟。先看模型假设 A1-A3 和 Proposition 1，公式很简单，但它把 reference-free judging 的边界讲清楚了。然后看 within-question estimator，因为这是论文里最可复用的测量设计。实验部分重点看三处：不同任务上 AUC 的分布，benchmark accuracy 到 grading-context competence 的分解，以及 closed-loop gate 的错误类型。如果关心 reward hacking 或自动数据筛选，可以细读 non-intervening probe 的协议：episode-level scoring、answer-content feeding、within-question stratification、clustered bootstrap 和 proxy-based decontamination。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks when a reference-free LLM judge can replace a verifier gate in text-space skill optimization. Instead of asking whether the judge is generally accurate, it asks whether the judge score can separate verifier-correct outputs from verifier-wrong outputs on the actual candidates produced during optimization.","importance":"The paper is not a core pretraining contribution, but it is useful for thinking about training-signal quality. LLM judges are often used for data filtering, reward construction, rejection sampling, and automatic evaluation. The key lesson is that benchmark accuracy is not the right deployment diagnostic. What matters is discriminability under the target task, candidate distribution, and grading context. This transfers directly to audits of reward models, synthetic-data filters, and judge-based SFT/RL pipelines.","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"The empirical results show large task dependence. Judge discriminability is near chance on research mathematics, but more usable on factual QA and graduate-level science tasks. Where difficulty confounding can be controlled, the closed-form competence-to-AUC relation matches the observed pattern. The paper also decomposes why headline benchmark accuracy overstates grading-context competence: censoring convention, benchmark exposure, and the solve-versus-grade context gap all push the estimate in the optimistic direction. In a closed-loop gating study, judges that fail the diagnostic import regressions, while a judge that passes the diagnostic imports no regressions and mainly makes over-rejection errors.","reading_guide":"A 25-35 minute skim is sufficient. In practice, however, models are often used to assist other (human or LLM) agents. The question that drives model selection is therefore not only which model produces the best output, but which model most improves the work of another (weaker) agent. We introduce a unified framework that evaluates the capability of models to automate and augment another agent's performance. Across seven economically grounded real-world tasks","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-19T05:22:20Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_18554.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CY","cs.AI","cs.MA","econ.GN"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文要解决的问题不是提升模型能力，而是重新定义一类更贴近真实使用方式的评测：很多工作场景里，模型并不是直接完成任务，而是给人类或较弱模型提供帮助。现有 benchmark 大多只测 automation，也就是模型独立产出最终结果的能力，却很少测 augmentation，也就是模型能否让另一个执行者做得更好。论文提出 CentaurBench，用统一框架在同一组真实工作任务上同时评估这两种能力，并检验二者是否一致。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接作用有限，它更偏评测与部署选择。但它有一个值得注意的启发：模型的“自己做得好”与“教别人做得好”不是同一种能力，二者相关性并不高。对于 post-train、teacher model、self-improvement pipeline、decomposition/critique 数据合成，这个结论是有用的，因为很多训练流程默认强模型天然也是好教师。CentaurBench 给出一个更细的视角：指导文本是否适合下游执行者、是否可操作、是否不过载，可能比 assistant 自身上限更重要。若把弱 worker 换成固定 student policy，这个框架也可看作 teacher quality eval。","technical_approach":"方法上，论文构造了一个双模式评测协议。对每个任务和每个候选模型，automation 模式下模型直接完成任务；augmentation 模式下，候选模型只输出 assistance text，由一个固定的低能力 worker model 根据这段帮助文本去完成任务。这样做的关键是把 executor 固定住，从而把最终结果中的差异尽量归因到 assistant 的指导质量，而不是团队里谁更强。评测覆盖 7 类有经济含义的真实工作任务。输出质量通过多位 LLM judge 按任务定制 rubric 做 blind pairwise comparison，并且避免同家族模型评自己家输出。最终得到的是一个按任务和模式分解的 rank matrix，而不是单一总榜。这个设计的核心优点是可比性强：同一任务、同一 worker、同一 judge protocol 下，可以直接比较“直接做”和“辅助别人做”两种能力。局限也很明确：augmentation 的结论依赖所选 worker model，测到的是‘对这个 worker 的帮助能力’，不是对所有人类或所有模型的普适帮助能力。","experiments":"关键结果有三点。第一，augmentation 与 automation 的模型排名只中等相关，整体 rank correlation 约为 0.48，说明二者不能互相替代。第二，这个相关性在任务间波动很大，从 travel planning 的 -0.04 到 tax preparation 的 0.85，表明任务结构会显著影响“好执行者”和“好助手”是否重合。第三，帮助并不总是有益。论文提到 GPT-5-mini 在 automation 和 augmentation 两种模式下平均都排第一，但在 augmentation 比较里，不带任何外部帮助、由 GPT-3.5-Turbo 单独完成任务的 worker baseline 反而拿到第二名平均排名。这说明一些 assistant 生成的指导可能过于复杂、和 worker 能力不匹配，或者在任务上校准失当，结果不如不帮。任务级 reversal 也很明显，例如 Claude-Opus-4.8 在 market trends analysis 上是最强 automation 模型之一，平均 rank 2.05，但在同任务 augmentation 上接近最弱，8.15；而 GPT-4.1 在 counseling 上则呈现相反画像。对读者来说，最值得看的不是谁排第一，而是这些 reversal 暗示了什么样的帮助文本更适合弱执行者。","reading_guide":"这是典型的评测论文，泛读即可。先看任务设计和双模式协议，确认 augmentation 是如何 operationalize 的；再看 judge 设计、盲评和排除自评的细节，判断结果可信度；最后看 task-level rank table 和相关性分析。预计 20 到 30 分钟足够。若只关心方法启发，重点看两处：一是固定 worker 的设计为什么能隔离 assistant 质量；二是哪些任务上 automation/augmentation 排名反转最大，这些任务通常更能暴露“会做”和“会教”的差异。若你做 pretrain，这篇不需要深读；若你做 teacher-student、self-play critique、AI delegation eval，可以把它当作一个有用的评测模板。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses an evaluation gap rather than a new modeling method. Most LLM benchmarks measure automation: how well a model completes a task by itself. In real deployments, however, models are often used to assist another agent, human or model, rather than replace it. CentaurBench asks a different question: which model most improves the performance of a weaker worker? It builds a unified benchmark that measures both automation and augmentation on the same set of real-world work tasks.","importance":"Its direct relevance to pretraining is limited, but it carries an important lesson for post-training and model-role design. A model that performs well as an autonomous solver is not necessarily a good teacher, coach, or critic for another agent. That matters for teacher-model selection, synthetic supervision pipelines, critique-based training, and delegation settings where a strong model is assumed to be a good guide by default. The benchmark makes that assumption testable. It also suggests that assistance quality depends on calibration to the downstream worker, not just raw model capability.","technical_approach":"The benchmark uses a two-mode protocol. In automation mode, the focal model completes the task end to end. In augmentation mode, the focal model produces assistance text only, and a fixed lower-capacity worker model uses that assistance to produce the final deliverable. Keeping the worker fixed is the key design choice: it isolates the marginal value of the assistant’s guidance from the executor’s own capability. The benchmark spans seven economically grounded professional tasks. Outputs are evaluated through blind pairwise comparisons by an LLM judge panel using task-specific rubrics, with family-level exclusions to avoid self-judging. The end product is a task-by-model rank matrix for both regimes rather than a single leaderboard. The main strength of the design is comparability across roles. The main limitation is external validity: augmentation performance is measured relative to one standardized worker, so it captures “helpfulness to this worker” rather than universal helpfulness.","experiments":"$21","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. Start with the benchmark design and make sure the augmentation protocol is clear. After that, go straight to the task-level rank tables and the automation-versus-augmentation correlation analysis. Around 20–30 minutes should be enough. The most useful questions to keep in mind are why fixing the worker isolates assistant quality, and which tasks produce the largest rank reversals.
