We argue that this dichotomy is incomplete and introduce geometric iterative retrieval, a ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-19T17:29:56Z","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2608_19141.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.SD","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是神经音频 codec 的 resynthesis 问题：已知第一层或粗层 RVQ token，怎样恢复后续残差层，才能把离散 token 重新解码成更高保真的音频。已有两条路各有明显缺陷：逐层离散分类尊重 RVQ 层级，但不利用 codebook 几何；一步连续回归利用几何，但容易均值化、过平滑，也缺少逐层纠错能力。作者要找的是第三条路：既保留 RVQ 的 coarse-to-fine 结构，又在连续 codebook 空间里做迭代预测。","importance":"对 LLM 基座研究的相关性来自语音/音频 LM 的离散化接口，而不是音频重建本身。很多语音 LM、AudioLM/VALL-E 一类系统都把 codec token 当语言建模对象；如果 resynthesis 端过弱，token 生成质量的上限会被 codec 还原器卡住。这篇工作的启发在于：离散 token 生成之后，未必只能做分类式后处理，也可以在 codebook embedding 空间里做 retrieval-style refinement。这个思路对多码本 token、分层离散表示、甚至视觉 tokenizer 的 coarse-to-fine 重建都有参考价值。不过它更偏 codec reconstruction，不是 foundation pretrain 主线。","technical_approach":"作者提出 geometric iterative retrieval。核心设计有三层。第一，预测目标不是离散 codebook index，也不是一次性回归完整 latent，而是逐层预测每个 RVQ 层对应的连续 codebook vector e_k。这样既保留了 RVQ 的层级语义，又能利用向量空间中的距离结构。第二，训练目标不是 MSE，而是 CLIP 风格的对比检索损失。对每个时间位置和每个层，模型输出的预测向量需要在 cosine 相似度上更接近真实 codebook 向量，而远离 batch 中其他负样本。这样做的动机很明确：离散分类把所有错误类别看成同样错，MSE 又会把预测往均值拉；contrastive retrieval 则更贴近“在连续空间里找最近正确码字”的目标。第三，作者不再沿用 RVQ 解码时简单逐层求和的条件方式，而是用 self-attention aggregator 对已知低层 embedding 做非加性聚合，让模型学习跨层依赖。实现上，骨干是 12 层、hidden size 1536 的 bidirectional DeBERTa-v3。训练时用沿 codebook 轴的 causal masking，在单次前向中同时学习所有高层预测；推理时则按层自回归，从第 2 层到第 D 层逐步预测，每一步把连续预测映射到最近 codebook entry，再作为下一层条件。这个设计和 diffusion resynthesis 的区别在于：它同样是 iterative continuous prediction，但迭代轴来自 codec 自身的层级，而不是外加的噪声时间表。","experiments":"实验集中在 DAC codec restoration，覆盖 speech 和 music。作者比较了几类基线：单步 token prediction、MSE regression、cosine regression，以及文中强调的 retrieval 式方法。结果上，geometric iterative retrieval 在 learned baseline 中取得最好的 LSD，并在双盲主观听测里更常被偏好。全文给出的定性结论很稳定：1）逐层连续检索优于一步连续回归，说明 iterative refinement 本身有价值；2）contrastive 目标优于 MSE/cosine 回归，说明 codebook 几何若通过检索式目标来利用，比直接做向量回归更合适；3）self-attention aggregation 优于简单加和条件，说明 RVQ 的“残差相加”适合作为 codec 结构，不一定适合作为预测器的条件归纳偏置。文中方法的推理步数是 D-1，与离散 coarse-to-fine 预测一致，没有引入 diffusion 那种更长的采样链。摘要里没有给出完整数字，全文重点也更偏相对比较而非大规模 scaling。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看 Table 1 和方法图，先把作者提出的二维设计空间看明白：离散/连续 × 单步/迭代。这个 framing 比具体模型更有启发。然后看 Method 里三个关键部件：per-layer continuous target、contrastive retrieval loss、self-attention aggregator。实验部分重点看 LSD 和主观听测，以及消融里 retrieval loss 和 aggregator 的贡献。若只想判断是否值得迁移到语音 LM 或多码本 tokenizer 场景，20-30 分钟足够；若要复现，需要再细看负样本构造、最近邻量化步骤和训练/推理不一致带来的误差。","authors_note":"ETH Zurich / DISCO 相关团队；从文中仓库链接可见为 ETH-DISCO 项目。具体作者名单在给定内容中未完整出现。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies neural audio codec resynthesis: given coarse RVQ tokens, how should one recover the remaining residual codebook layers so that the final decoded audio has higher fidelity? Existing choices are unsatisfying. Layer-wise token prediction respects the RVQ hierarchy but ignores geometry in codebook space. One-shot continuous regression uses geometry but tends to average, oversmooth, and lacks iterative correction. The paper targets the missing quadrant: iterative prediction in continuous codebook space.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research comes through the tokenizer interface for speech and audio LM. Many speech generation systems model codec tokens with language-model-style architectures, and the quality ceiling is partly set by how well those tokens can be resynthesized. The useful idea here is that post-token generation need not be framed only as discrete classification; it can be cast as retrieval-style refinement in codebook embedding space. That is potentially interesting for multi-codebook tokenizers, hierarchical discrete representations, and even some vision-tokenizer settings. Still, this is more about codec reconstruction than core pretraining.","technical_approach":"$23","experiments":"$24","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the design-space table and the main method figure. Given a background, several transparent visual assets, and a text brief, the model must produce a coherent composition while preserving each asset exactly. Existing pipelines usually ask an LLM to predict discrete bounding boxes and then paste assets into those boxes. This separates spatial planning from rendering, introduces coordinate quantization errors, and prevents the planner from observing how the final canvas will actually look.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model pretraining is not the design application itself, but the modeling lesson: a pretrained diffusion transformer appears to contain usable continuous layout priors that can be read out without converting 2D composition into 1D coordinate tokens. This is useful for thinking about multimodal modeling, especially the boundary between autoregressive symbolic planning and diffusion-based visual synthesis. The engineering split is also sensible: use the diffusion model for global composition, and use deterministic post-processing for pixel-level asset fidelity.","technical_approach":"The system has two stages. First, a pretrained image-editing diffusion transformer is adapted with a small LoRA selected through a knockout-style procedure. The background, RGBA foreground assets, and text brief are fed into the model, with the visual elements entering the joint attention stream as ordinary visual tokens. No ground-truth bounding boxes are used during training; the model learns p(y | elements, brief), and the layout emerges during image generation. The authors report that additional conditioning modules from prior multi-element generation systems did not help, so the final design is deliberately minimal. Second, a deterministic Match-and-Place module uses a vision-language model to locate where each input element appeared in the generated draft, then pastes the original RGBA asset back at that location as a separate layer. The diffusion model supplies the layout; the post-processor supplies exact identity preservation and editability.","experiments":"Experiments are conducted on PrismLayersPlus. The main reported finding is that the produced designs are closer to ground-truth designs in perceived quality than both an LLM-based layout planner and a specialized layout transformer. A notable ablation is that more elaborate multi-element conditioning components do not improve performance over the simple LoRA adaptation. The Match-and-Place stage addresses a separate failure mode: stochastic diffusion sampling cannot reliably preserve logos, product cutouts, or text assets at the pixel level, while deterministic re-placement gives exact asset preservation and a layered editable output. The provided content does not include complete numeric tables, so the size of the gains should be read from the original experimental section.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 15–20 minutes. The authors isolate this with an oracle-routed benchmark built on SmolLM3-3B-Base, using three domains—math, code, and instruction following—so routing ambiguity is removed from the analysis. That is relevant for verifier-guided post-training, long-context reasoning, and distillation setups where local fluency and global correctness come apart.","technical_approach":"The proposed method, Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (GC-OPD), keeps the original OPD signal but calibrates it using verifier feedback in a structured way. For each rollout group, the method separately normalizes verifier rewards and trajectory-level OPD scores, then takes their difference as a signed residual that measures teacher–verifier disagreement. Instead of adding verifier reward directly at the sequence level, the residual is distributed back to tokens with RACA, which uses relative OPD advantages to assign more calibration to tokens that matter more under the teacher signal. 把采样交互组织成稀疏树，并按时间反向优化各轮策略。关键设计是为某一轮构造 sibling continuations，在匹配的后续条件下估计该轮 advantage。这样做的目的有三个：减少 rollout 上下文和训练上下文不一致；让终局奖励通过后续 continuation 更有因果地回传到早期决策；控制短轨迹和长轨迹在不同 policy version 下训练造成的异步漂移。论文也将 The main issue is not just longer sequences. Flattening an interaction into one token trajectory creates a mismatch between the decision structure and the optimization objective. Rollout contexts, training-time likelihood-ratio contexts, sparse terminal rewards, and asynchronous updates of short and long trajectories interact in ways that become worse as the number of turns grows.","importance":"The work is mostly a post-training and agent-RL stability paper, not a core pretraining paper. Its relevance to foundation-model research is through the pretrain-to-posttrain interface. If long-horizon tool use, search, self-correction, and iterative reasoning are trained with RL, the unit of optimization matters. A longer context window alone does not solve long-horizon learning; state definition, credit assignment, and on-policy continuation become part of the training signal quality.","technical_approach":"RTPO models a multi-turn interaction as a hierarchical MDP. A turn-level state contains the prompt plus previous model responses and environment feedback, while a turn-level action is the current response. The response itself is still generated autoregressively at the token level. Unlike PPO, GRPO, or GSPO-style methods that optimize a flattened trajectory, RTPO arranges sampled interactions as sparse reverse trees and optimizes turn policies in reverse temporal order. For each turn, it constructs sibling continuations so that the turn-level advantage is estimated under matched downstream conditions. This is intended to address three coupled problems: rollout-training context inconsistency, weak turn-level credit under terminal rewards, and policy drift caused by asynchronous optimization of trajectories of different lengths. The paper also positions TreeGRPO, ARPO, and SeeUPO as partial fixes that do not fully align advantage estimates with each turn’s causal contribution to the continuation.","experiments":"The experiments target multi-turn agentic RL, especially tool-integrated reasoning workflows. The provided full-content excerpt does not include concrete benchmark names, model sizes, training budgets, or numerical gains, so the safe reading is qualitative. Our analysis framewor","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-18T19:42:16Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_18271.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在 on-policy self-distillation（同一底座模型做 teacher/student）里，teacher 端加入“特权参考解”（reference solution）到底是否真的把参考信息教给了 student，还是只是把底座模型本来就具备但未被 student 当前策略激活的推理行为“恢复/蒸馏”出来。","importance":"对 post-train ↔ pretrain 闭环的启发点很明确：\n- OPSD 的收益不能简单归因于“reference 提供了额外知识”。在一些设置下，正确 reference 不是必要条件，甚至错题的 reference 也可能更有效。这提示很多“用更强 teacher/更多上下文”带来的提升，可能主要来自分布/模式迁移（例如把 thinking mode 的行为蒸馏到 non-thinking student），而不是信息注入。\n- 论文给了一个可操作的分析分解：把 teacher supervision 拆成 reference-induced 与 recovery 两个方向，并用对齐度量去解释训练中 student 预测分布的变化。这个框架对分析 RL/OPD 类方法的训练信号来源很有用，能帮助判断“训练到底在学什么”。\n整体属于值得泛读的机制分析型工作；它不直接改进预训练，但能减少对 OPSD 机制的误判，从而影响后训练 recipe 的选择。","technical_approach":"方法上做了两件事：实验控制 + 监督信号分解。\n- 实验控制：固定 student 始终为 non-thinking 模式（训练与评测都不变），只在 teacher 侧切换 generation mode（thinking vs non-thinking）以及 reference 条件（正确参考/错误参考/无参考/来自其他题的参考）。这样把“teacher 更会想”与“teacher 看到了参考”两种因素拆开。\n- 信号分解：对 student 在某个 checkpoint 生成的轨迹 y，在每个 token 位置比较三种 next-token 分布：\n  1) Teacher with reference: T\n  2) Teacher without reference: N\n  3) Student at checkpoint c: S_c\n  用 log-prob 向量差分把总监督方向分解为：\n  - D_ref = (T - N)：reference 引入的方向（reference-induced supervision）\n  - D_rec = (N - S_c)：不靠 reference 的“恢复方向”（recovery），可理解为 teacher 在同样上下文下相对 student 的差异\n  - D_tot = (T - S_c) = D_ref + D_rec\n  然后度量 D_ref 在 D_tot 上的分量/对齐程度，并跟踪训练中 student 预测变化与这些方向的对齐。\n- 控制对照：用“其他问题的 reference/context”构造 controls，检验看似的对齐是否只是分布性伪相关。\n这套设计的关键点是：不把 performance gain 直接当作“学到了 reference”，而是用分布几何分解去追踪训练信号来源。","experiments":"实验设置（从正文可见）：\n- 模型：Qwen3 系列 1.7B / ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-19T05:05:15Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_18545.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究的是多语言模型内部机制共享的边界：同一种语法操作在不同语言里是否由同一组电路实现，以及这种共享是否取决于该语法现象在表层上是否被显式表达。作者选了 present-tense subject-verb agreement 作为切口，因为它在语言类型学上差异很大：很多语言有明显的人称/数形态变化，英语只保留一部分，中文几乎没有。核心问题不是模型会不会做主谓一致，而是当它做这件事时，跨语言是否调用了共享 attention heads，还是各语言各自形成了独立解法。","importance":"这不是直接服务于训练 recipe 的论文，但对 multilingual pretraining 很有信息量。它给出的启发是：跨语言参数共享不是均匀发生的，是否共享取决于底层语法结构是否真的对齐、以及这种结构在训练语料里是否有足够稳定的表层信号。对预训练研究者，这会影响三类判断：一是多语数据混合时哪些能力更可能正迁移；二是英语中心的机制解释能否迁移到其他语言；三是 tokenizer、语料覆盖和语言配比会怎样影响共享电路的形成。贡献量级属于机制分析里的扎实实证，不是训练方法创新，但它把“共享表示”这个常见口号落到了可因果检验的 head-level 证据上。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者在 29 种语言、五个开源模型家族、六个 checkpoint 上做 activation patching 和 attention analysis。实验设计比较克制，重点是控制变量。数据来自 UniMorph 等形态资源，先抽取动词原形及其现在时屈折形式，再用统一模板构造 zero-shot prompt，例如“Conjugation of the verb X in present tense: pronoun conjugation”，并针对各语言做适配。这样做牺牲了自然语境，但换来了 clean/corrupted prompt 的位置对齐，便于做 patching。作者区分了两种恢复指标：一种是 broader target-form recovery，即 patch 某个 head 后，模型是否更能恢复上下文正确的目标词；另一种是更严格的 minimal-pair inflection recovery，即只看它是否恢复了屈折对比本身。然后在各语言中识别对 agreement 有因果作用的 attention heads，再比较这些 head 集合在不同语言间的相似度。论文的关键判断不只是“有没有 overlap”，而是 overlap 是否随着 overt inflection 的强弱系统变化。结果显示，在显式标记人称/数的一组语言里，共享电路更明显；英语是桥接案例，它在 third-person singular 这类仍有屈折的情形上更接近强屈折语言，而在无屈折情形上相似度较低。作者还检查了这些重叠 heads 的 attention pattern，发现它们不仅位置重合，功能角色也相近，更像共享信息路由，而不只是偶然共址。","experiments":"实验覆盖 29 种语言、五个模型家族、六个 checkpoint；初始词汇库存跨 34 种语言，单语言动词条目数从 56 到 23,782 不等，平均约 1,750。作者选了四类人称/数对比：1sg↔2sg、1sg↔3sg、1pl↔3pl、3sg↔3pl，其中英语只有两类存在屈折变化。主要结果有三点。第一，显式主谓一致语言之间的 agreement circuitry 有显著部分共享，尤其在 inflection-specific 的 minimal-pair 指标下更明显。第二，缺少人称屈折的语言与这组共享电路的相似度明显更低，说明共享不是“多语言模型天然统一”，而是跟共享语法结构和表层实现强相关。第三，instruction tuning、模型家族和规模会影响绝对模式，但不改变主结论。给出的全文片段没有完整数值表，因此不宜编造具体相似度百分比或 head 数量；真正精读时应回原文看各模型 family 的 similarity reuse the same internal circuitry across languages, and when they do not. The test case is present-tense subject-verb agreement, which is ideal because the same grammatical operation is realized very differently across languages: many languages mark person and number overtly, English marks only part of it, and Chinese largely does not. The central question is not whether models can produce the right verb form, but whether the causal mechanism behind that behavior is shared across languages or implemented by language-specific circuits.","importance":"This is not a training-method paper, but it is highly relevant to multilingual pretraining. The main lesson is that parameter sharing does not imply uniform mechanistic sharing. Cross-lingual overlap depends on whether the underlying grammatical structure is genuinely aligned and whether it is stably expressed in the training signal. That matters for at least three reasons: it affects which capabilities are likely to transfer under multilingual data mixing; it limits how far English-centric interpretability results can generalize; and it suggests that tokenizer design, corpus composition, and language balance may shape whether shared circuits emerge at all. The contribution is a careful mechanistic study rather than a new model, but it grounds the vague idea of “shared representations” in causal head-level evidence.","technical_approach":"$2c","experiments":"$2d","reading_guide":"A useful skim starts with the introduction, because the paper is careful about the question it is asking: not whether transfer exists, but when shared circuitry emerges. After that, go straight to the main result figures: the head-level similarity matrices across languages, the contrast between broad recovery and minimal-pair recovery, and the English case split by inflecting versus non-inflecting contrasts. A 20 to 30 minute read is enough to get the main point. ESMFold pLDDT 只有 0.35。论文还发现 steered activations 会落到与随机氨基酸输入统计上难以区分的区域。Mahalanobis 过滤在匹配 steering 设置下改善 property–structure trade-off，计算代价低，但它只是筛选层，不能从根本上修复生成器或 oracle 的外推问题。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 过滤的适用范围和失败边界。若关心 LLM post-train，可把它当作一个小型可控案例：当优化目标来自冻结 predictor 时，表示流形监控可能比单看 reward/oracle 更可靠。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a failure mode of inference-time guidance in protein language models: when guidance is strong enough to improve the target property score, the model’s internal representations can leave the natural protein manifold, producing low-complexity and poorly foldable sequences that are still rewarded by the optimized oracle.","importance":"The direct relevance to LLM pretraining is limited, but the control problem is familiar. A frozen generative prior is pushed by an external signal, and the optimized score can improve while samples move out of distribution. This is a useful analogue for activation steering, classifier guidance, reward-model optimization, and reward hacking in language models. The main lesson is that monitoring only the optimized objective can miss representation-level collapse.","technical_approach":"The main setting is ESM-2 650M with iterative masked decoding. Activation steering uses a direction computed from the difference between mean activations of high-property and low-property reference sets, then injects this direction into the residual stream during decoding. The main experiments use all-layer injection. Although the update rescales the intervention to preserve the raw activation norm, the paper argues that this does not preserve the whitened per-channel activation profile, so activations can still become atypical. The proposed mitigation is Mahalanobis-filtered steering: estimate natural activation statistics, run a one-sided χ²-inspired typicality test, and filter generated samples after decoding. The authors also test predictor-gradient guidance to show that the representation collapse is not tied to one specific steering mechanism.","experiments":"The experiments target solubility and thermostability. The property oracles are a solubility classifier and a T_m regressor trained on frozen ESM-2 features. Structural plausibility is measured at the population level using ESMFold pLDDT. Under strong steering, oracle scores increase, but activation typicality deteriorates, ESMFold confidence drops, and sequences become low-complexity. One example is a glycine homopolymer assigned a solubility score of 0.99 while its ESMFold pLDDT is only 0.35. The authors report that guided activations can move toward a region statistically indistinguishable from random amino-acid input. Mahalanobis filtering improves the property–structure trade-off at matched guidance settings with little extra cost, but it remains a screening method rather than a fix for oracle extrapolation or generator mismatch.","reading_guide":"A skim should take about 25–35 minutes. Read Section 2 for the exact steering mechanism and why norm preservation is not enough. Section 3 contains the main evidence for off-manifold collapse: activation typicality, low-complexity degeneration, pLDDT degradation, and the mismatch with oracle scores. cluster-wise score 的 posterior-weighted average，于是去噪过程可以解释成一个随时间演化的 Bayesian classifier：在高噪声阶段，后验较平，样本在簇之间探索；当信噪比升到某个阈值后，后验迅速集中，轨迹以高概率“承诺”到单一簇。文中给出的阈值量级是 SNR 达到 Θ(log(KD)/D)。第二，在误差分析上，作者把去噪过程按这个 cluster commitment 时间点切成前后两段：前段处理跨簇混合带来的误差，后段在单簇内分析局部低维结构。这样可以把 KL 误差上界从依赖环境维度 D，改写成主要依赖各簇最大 intrinsic dimension，并只保留对 D 的对数因子依赖。论文的关键区别在于，它不是只分析单流形或单高斯，也不是只给出定性三阶段图景，而是把“多簇相变动力学”和“KL 误差维度缩减”放进同一个严格证明框架。代价也很明显：假设较强，主要建立在 GMM、簇间分离、正则性条件和可控的离散化误差之上。","experiments":"这篇稿件的重点是理论，不是大规模经验实验。全文给出的主要结果是定理、命题和相图式数值示意，而不是预训练规模 benchmark。关键结论包括：1）当簇数 K 随 D 多项式增长时，只要满足分离条件，去噪轨迹仍会在 SNR≈Θ(log(KD)/D) 附近完成簇选择；2）在适当正则条件下，DDPM 的 KL 误差上界对维度的主导项与最大簇内在维度线性相关，而不是与环境维度 D 线性相关；3）这种分析把早期跨簇探索和后期簇内恢复分开处理，因此能解释为什么多模态并不必然破坏低维适应性。若从阅读收益看，数值部分主要用于验证相变位置和理论量级，而不是展示某个模型在标准生成任务上的提升幅度。","reading_guide":"建议把精力放在三处。第一是引言后半段和 Section 3.1，那里最清楚地说明了“cluster commitment 阈值”和“KL 假设；否则很容易高估结论的普适性。预计 45-60 分钟可以抓住主线，若要判断能否迁移到 diffusion LM 或离散 token 空间，需要再细读证明中的几何条件。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise question: for diffusion models trained on high-dimensional multimodal data where each cluster has its own low-dimensional structure, does the denoising error scale with the ambient dimension D, or can it adapt to the intrinsic dimension of each cluster? The authors study this in a tractable Gaussian-mixture setting and connect two threads that are often separate: phase-transition-style denoising dynamics across clusters, and quantitative DDPM error bounds.","importance":"Its value is mainly conceptual and theoretical. It does not propose a new training recipe for foundation models, but it sharpens the picture of why diffusion can behave better than naive ambient-dimension bounds suggest on structured data. That matters for diffusion LM, discrete diffusion, and multimodal token modeling, where the data geometry is also clustered and locally low-dimensional. The contribution is best read as a mechanism paper: when and why diffusion can become intrinsic-dimension adaptive despite multimodality.","technical_approach":"$2e","experiments":"The paper is theory-first rather than benchmark-driven. The main results are theorems, propositions, and supporting numerical illustrations rather than large-scale empirical training runs. The central findings are: 1) even when the number of clusters K grows polynomially with D, the reverse trajectory still commits to one cluster around an SNR threshold of order Theta(log(KD)/D), provided separation assumptions hold; 2) under suitable regularity conditions, the KL error bound scales with the largest cluster intrinsic dimension rather than the ambient dimension; 3) separating the reverse process into pre-commitment and post-commitment phases explains how multimodality and intrinsic-dimension adaptivity can coexist. However, progress is fundamentally limited by the reliance on full-length trajectory imitation. For tasks involving multiple order-independent sub-goals, the optimal solution space forms a vast combinatorial diamond lattice. Forcing this rich topology into monolithic trajectories causes a severe topological collapse, indiscriminately penalizing valid alternative explorati","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-19T04:19:21Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_18524.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG","cs.MA"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是多轮 tool-calling agent 蒸馏里的一个结构性问题：现有 SFT/BC 把教师轨迹当成单条线性序列去模仿，RL 又常把奖励粗糙地摊到整条轨迹上，但很多真实任务包含多个可交换顺序的子目标，正确解空间不是一条路径，而是大量在中间状态汇合、再分叉的“菱形”状态图。在线性监督下，学生模型只要走了与教师不同但同样有效的前缀，就会被当成错误覆盖；在 RL 下，失败轨迹中的有效中间步骤也容易被误罚。论文要解决的核心问题，就是如何在这种多解、可交换子目标的环境里，避免拓扑信息塌缩，保留有效前缀，只在真正偏离成功区域的位置做纠偏。","importance":"对基座预训练本身，这不是直接改 pretrain recipe 的论文，但它对“序列建模目标如何匹配真实解空间结构”有明确启发。它指出：当监督对象天然是图结构或部分有序结构时，强行压成单一 next-step imitation 会带来系统性偏差。这一点不只适用于 agent 蒸馏，也会影响 tool-use 数据合成、过程监督、甚至更广义的 any-order / graph-structured reasoning 学习。贡献量级更接近 post-train / agent training 中一个扎实的方法改进，而不是范式级变化；价值主要在于把 credit assignment 问题从“奖励设计”进一步推进到“状态空间拓扑建模”。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 DART-SD，分三步。第一步，用教师 rollout 构建 Interaction-State Transition Graph。它不把节点定义成单步 action，而是定义成“累计交互状态”，也就是到当前为止已经获得了哪些任务相关信息。为此作者先做 information atom abstraction：把工具返回结果规范化，抽取任务可用的信息原子；语义等价的返回映射到同一 atom，空返回、状态码、not-found 之类无信息响应被折叠。这样，不同调用顺序但获得相同信息集合的轨迹会落到同一状态节点上，天然表达 order-independent sub-goals 的 diamond topology。第二步，对学生失败轨迹做 success-reachable projection，找到 Critical Topological Breakpoint。做法是把学生每个累计状态投影到 ISTG 中“仍可到达成功”的区域，定位最后一个仍处于成功可达区、以及之后首次脱离该区域的位置。这个 CTB 比传统 hindsight 更细，因为它不是按时间步切分，而是按是否还在成功拓扑内切分。第三步，从 CTB 的恢复锚点出发，在 ISTG 中检索一条 success-supported continuation，生成局部恢复监督，只对恢复段计算损失，前缀保持不动。训练是 progressive self-distillation：学生反复 rollout，暴露新的能力边界，再做 CTB-guided localized supervision，逐步延长有效前缀。与普通 SFT 的区别在于监督局部化；与 RL 的区别在于不依赖稀疏回报反传，而是显式利用教师图结构做结构化纠偏。","experiments":"实验覆盖五个 benchmark、两个模型尺度，论文主结论是 DART-SD 相比 supervised baseline 和 RL baseline 都更稳，且能减少冗余 tool calls、延长 valid interaction prefix。给出的全文片段里没有完整结果表和精确数字，因此没法负责任地复述具体绝对分数、提升幅度、参数规模和数据规模。就定性结果看，作者重点证明了四件事：一是在线性 imitation 容易误罚的多子目标任务上，DART-SD 的成功率更高；二是学生在失败轨迹中往往已有较长正确前缀，局部恢复比全轨迹重写更高效；三是 ISTG 若不用累计状态、而退回 action-level 表示，效果会下降，说明状态抽象确实在解决顺序交换问题；四是 progressive self-distillation 比单轮纠偏更有效，说明学生能力边界会随着训练推进而外移。若要真正使用这篇论文，建议回到原文表格核对每个 benchmark 的绝对提升、工具调用次数变化、以及不同模型规模下收益是否一致。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1/2，抓住“topological collapse”这个问题定义，再看 Method 里 ISTG、information atom abstraction、CTB 的定义。最值得看的不是 agent 任务本身，而是作者如何把多轨迹监督改写成状态图上的局部恢复问题。若时间有限，优先看：1）ISTG 节点为什么用累计交互状态而不是 action；2）success-reachable projection 如何区分 harmless exploration 和 fatal deviation；3）ablation 里去掉 localized supervision、去掉 progressive distillation、或替换状态抽象后的性能变化。预计 25–35 分钟能完成一次有效泛读；若要复现或迁移到 tool-use SFT 数据构造，再细看信息原子抽取和检索式恢复生成细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a structural failure mode in distilling multi-turn tool-calling agents. Standard BC/SFT treats teacher behavior as a single linear trajectory, while RL often spreads credit too coarsely across the whole rollout. But many tool-use tasks contain order-independent sub-goals, so the valid solution space is not a line: it is a graph with many alternative paths that merge at shared intermediate states. Under linear supervision, a student can take a valid but different prefix and still be penalized. Under standard RL, useful intermediate actions inside failed trajectories are often punished as well. The core problem is how to preserve valid prefixes and correct only the true deviation point, instead of collapsing a graph-structured solution space into one forced sequence.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it is relevant to foundation-model training because it makes a clean point about objective mismatch: when the target behavior is naturally graph-structured or partially ordered, next-step imitation can introduce systematic bias. That lesson matters for agent distillation, tool-use data construction, process supervision, and more broadly for any setting where multiple action orders are semantically equivalent. The contribution is best viewed as a solid post-training / agent-training method rather than a new modeling paradigm. Its main value is the shift from generic credit assignment to topology-aware credit assignment.","technical_approach":"$2f","experiments":"$30","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the introduction and the main overview figure. The key idea is the definition of topological collapse. Then read the method sections on the ISTG, information atom abstraction, and CTB. The most useful part is not the agent application itself, but the reframing of supervision from trajectory imitation to local recovery on a state graph. If time is limited, focus on three questions: why cumulative interaction states are better than action-level nodes; how success-reachable projection separates harmless exploration from fatal deviation; and what the ablations say when localized supervision, progressive distillation, or the state abstraction is removed. library 和 evaluator agent，测试是否只是缺少经验；二是在训练开始前加入 human reviewer 修改初始策略，测试是否只是缺少指导；三是增加推理 token，测试是否只是 reasoning compute 不足。","experiments":"分析覆盖 1,338 条公开 post-training 轨迹，来自 PostTrainBench 的 7 个 lock-in：策略基本在最开始确定，后续预算主要花在同一策略内的局部修正。这个锁定更多反映 agent 先验，而不是任务差异。比如 Claude Code 轨迹中 80.7% 收敛到 full-parameter SFT，Codex CLI 轨迹中 89.6% 收敛到 PEFT。增强 scaffold 后，执行效果提升明显，在 GSM8K 上提升 12.6 分，在 HumanEval 上提升 40.8 分，但策略仍然不变。human reviewer 可以在训练前重定向初始策略，但训练启动后 agent 仍回到局部调整循环。增强版 agent 消耗 2–8 倍推理 token，在较容易任务上有效，但在最难任务上几乎没有收益。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 Introduction、Preliminaries、轨迹分析部分和干预实验部分，约 30–40 分钟。重点看 verified training experiment 的计数规则。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks what current LLM agents lack when they are used to post-train other LLMs: they can execute a chosen training plan, but they rarely revise the plan after seeing experimental evidence.","importance":"The contribution is an empirical diagnosis of automated AI R&D, with direct implications for post-training and for the pretrain–posttrain feedback loop. The key distinction is between execution-level capability and strategy-level capability. Current agents can write code, launch training, debug scripts, tune hyperparameters, and improve metrics. What they fail to do is reconsider the high-level training strategy, such as switching between full SFT, PEFT, RL, data redesign, or a different stage structure. This matters because large gains in training often come from changing the objective, data regime, or stage design, not from local search inside a poor initial plan.","technical_approach":"The study analyzes complete public trajectories from PostTrainBench. It defines a training strategy as the high-level choice of training paradigm, data regime, and stage structure, while the training pipeline is the concrete implementation in code, data, and runs. The authors reconstruct tool calls, shell commands, file edits, command outputs, training jobs, and evaluation results, then classify whether an agent is executing within a fixed strategy or changing the strategy itself. They also test three explanations for the failure mode: lack of experience, lack of guidance, and insufficient reasoning compute. The corresponding interventions are an experiment journal, a skill library, a separate evaluator agent, a human reviewer before training starts, and larger inference-token budgets.","experiments":"$31","reading_guide":"Skim the Introduction, Preliminaries, the trajectory analysis, and the intervention experiments. Which skill to read has thus become a decision the policy itself makes in the middle of an episode, yet no existing signal trains it. We show that the default remedy, outcome-rewarded RL over the candidate slate, cannot teach it, for a structural reason we identify and name selector credit starvation: under a broadcast, sequence-leve","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-19T12:24:03Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_18852.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是长时程 agent 中一个被低估但很实际的问题：当技能库很大、技能只能按需读取时，policy 需要在轨迹中途先从候选技能名和简介里选一个再继续执行。现有做法通常把“选对技能”和“后续执行好任务”都放进同一个 outcome-only RL 信号里训练。论文指出这在结构上就不成立，尤其在长轨迹里更明显。原因不是 reward 稀疏这么简单，而是 skill selection 这一步只对应极少数 token，却继承整条轨迹的 broadcast advantage，导致两类问题同时出现：一是 loss 权重占比随轨迹变长迅速稀释；二是 credit sign 经常错，因为技能选对了，但后续执行失败时，选择技能的 token 反而会被负向更新。作者把这个现象定义为 selector credit starvation，并把它作为 in-policy skill selection 学不会的主要原因。","importance":"对基座研究的直接价值不在 agent 应用本身，而在 credit assignment 的拆分方式。它给出一个很清楚的例子：当一个序列里混有不同语义层级的决策时，用统一的 sequence-level advantage 去训练会系统性污染局部关键决策的梯度。这个问题和长链条 tool use、检索决策、router token、latent action、甚至多阶段 reasoning 中的局部选择训练都有共通性。论文的贡献主要有三层。第一，提出并量化了一个结构性失败模式，而不是把问题归因于模型能力不足。第二，给出一种 token-support 级别的 credit partition 方法，把“选择”与“执行”分开训练。第三，实验说明这种拆分在长时程 agent RL 里能带来稳定收益。对做 RL pretrain、tool-augmented LM、decision token training 的人，这个思路是有迁移价值的。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 SkillGate：在同一个 policy-gradient 更新里，显式构造两个互不重叠的 credit channel。任务通道仍然使用常规的 group-normalized outcome advantage，但把整个 skill-read tool call 从这个通道的支持集里移除，也就是任务成败不再直接回传到“读哪个技能”的那些 token。选择通道则只作用在命名所选技能的 token span 上，使用 action-local advantage。这个 advantage 不是由最终任务结果直接给出，而是由选择动作本身是否正确来定义：当轨迹只发起一次 read，且读到的是 oracle 标注的正确技能时给正信号；然后在 prompt group 内做 centering，减少组间难度差异带来的偏置。论文还强调两个工程细节。其一是 rollout-time span attribution，需要在采样时精确记录 skill-read 对应的 token span，保证 credit support 真正可分。其二是 length-invariant reweighting：两个通道在 batch 内按相等损失权重处理，并让每个 read action 分到相等份额，从而避免选择决策的训练强度随着轨迹长度下降。和直接做 finer-grained reward shaping 不同，这里不是把同一个 reward 切得更细，而是承认“选技能”和“执行任务”是两种证据来源不同的决策，应该用不同目标来学。和把 skill selection 视作 retrieval 预处理也不同，SkillGate 训练的是 policy 内部、episode 中途发生的 in-policy choice。","experiments":"论文先做了失败机理审计，再做方法验证。机理部分基于一次标准 outcome-only RL 训练 run 的 12,800 条 on-policy 轨迹，给出三组关键量化结果。第一，share：命名所选技能的 token 在整条轨迹中的中位占比只有 0.14%，而且轨迹越长，占比越低。第二，sign：约 40% 的这些 token 收到负 advantage；在最长轨迹上，即便技能选对，受到惩罚的频率也高于被奖励。第三，value：在 matched prompt groups 内，选对技能对任务成功率仍有 +11.2 个百分点的价值，说明这不是“无关紧要的小决策”，而是高价值但低信噪比的决策。方法验证部分覆盖 5 个 agent benchmark。摘要里给出的代表性结果是 trial success 从 47.0% 提升到 53.2%。从论文叙述看，收益主要集中在长时程、技能候选相似、且后续执行噪声较大的场景，这和作者提出的 starvation 诊断是一致的。消融重点应当包括：只做局部 advantage 不做通道分离、只做通道分离不做长度重加权、以及不同 selector utility 定义的比较。论文主张真正起作用的是三件事同时成立：支持集不重叠、selector signal 只看选择正确性、以及长度无关的损失分配。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓两部分。第一是引言里对 selector credit starvation 的定义和三项诊断指标，这部分决定了整篇文章是否成立。第二是方法部分关于 disjoint credit channels 的形式化定义，尤其要看 skill-read span 如何从 task channel 中剔除，以及 selector advantage 如何构造。若时间有限，优先看图 1 或 teaser、方法主公式、以及展示 share/sign/value 随 horizon 变化的图表，再看主结果表。预计 25 到 35 分钟能把核心思想读明白。更值得细看的点有两个：一是作者为什么坚持“分离 credit”而不是“改 reward”；二是 oracle 正确技能标签在训练中的角色，这决定了方法能否迁移到没有明确 gold skill 的场景。若你关心 RL for LM，更建议把它当作一个 token-level credit assignment 案例来读，而不是单纯的 agent 技能论文。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a specific failure mode in long-horizon agents with large skill libraries: the policy must decide, mid-trajectory, which skill file to open based only on a short name and description. Standard outcome-only RL treats this choice and all subsequent execution under one sequence-level advantage. The paper argues that this setup is structurally unable to train the selector well. The skill-choice tokens occupy a tiny fraction of the trajectory, so their gradient share vanishes as trajectories get longer. Worse, their credit sign is often wrong: a correct skill choice is penalized whenever later execution fails. The paper names this phenomenon selector credit starvation and frames it as the main reason in-policy skill selection remains weak under vanilla agent RL.","importance":"Its value for pretraining and foundation-model research is the credit-assignment lesson, not the agent application alone. The paper shows a clean case where heterogeneous decisions inside one sequence should not share the same training signal. That insight transfers to tool-use policies, retrieval decisions, router tokens, latent actions, and multi-stage reasoning where a few local decisions have high downstream value but poor token mass. The contribution is threefold: it identifies a structural optimization failure rather than a capability gap, quantifies the failure on real training traces, and proposes a token-support partitioning scheme that improves learning. For researchers working on RL for LMs, decision-token training, or long-horizon policy optimization, this is a useful design pattern.","technical_approach":"$32","experiments":"$33","reading_guide":"For a quick read, focus on two parts.
